\title{Your Group I, II, III and \texttt{4rho.tex}: every step checked;\\ the terms $\propto\log\frac{k^+}{\Lambda}$ (JIMWLK) and $\propto\log\frac{\vee}{k^+}$ (BFKL), row by row;\\ three- and four-$\rho$ cancellation; the two-$\rho$ sums and your two formulas}

\noindent Notation as in your files: $\mathcal K(X)=X/X^2$, $K(a,b,z)=\mathcal K(a-z)\cdot\mathcal K(b-z)$, $U^{bc}=U^{cb}$,
\begin{equation*}
\mathcal N=\frac{1}{(2\pi)^3}\frac{g^4}{16\pi^5}\frac{1}{k^+}.
\end{equation*}
Groups I and III are taken with their complex conjugate, Group II without, in every test (three $\rho$, two $\rho$, poles). \new{Blue}: new or corrected; \old{red}: removed. Only $\vee>k^+$ and $p^+>\Lambda$ are used; $\Lambda\to0$ wherever possible, $\vee$ exact, and every step is an exact identity. The remaining terms, the running coupling, DGLAP and the finite terms are in the companion file \texttt{Remaining\_RC\_DGLAP\_finite.pdf}.

\section{Answer}\label{sec:answer}
\paragraph{Your files.} Every step of \texttt{Group\_I.tex}, \texttt{Group\_II.tex}, \texttt{Group\_III.tex} and every equation of \texttt{4rho.tex} was checked numerically (Sec.~\ref{sec:files}). E1 (Group I Row V 2b UV) is now correct in your file. What is left to change:
\begin{description}
\item[M2] Group II Row IV, Eq.~(1.16): phase of the second term (typo; the next equation is right).
\item[4$\rho$] \texttt{4rho.tex}, Eq.~(1.12) (two $\rho$, first part): the factor $\new{\big[\log\frac{k^+}{\Lambda}+\log\frac{\vee}{k^+}\big]}$ is missing (the earlier file had $\log\frac{\vee}{\Lambda}$); F1 and F2 are still needed in the two-$\rho$ lists.
\item[F3] Group I Row III $d^3N_{3a}$: the logarithmic line with the edge kernel is replaced by $\mathcal D$. Without it the three-$\rho$ terms $\propto\log\frac{k^+}{\Lambda}$ do not cancel ($0.59$).
\item[C5] Group III Row III part 2: the measure is $\int_{w,x',y',z,x}$ (M4).
\item[M3, M5, M6] a prefactor sign in an unnumbered first line, a measure, typos (Sec.~\ref{sec:files}).
\item[M2'] Your JIMWLK formula: $M$ at the wrong points and the sign of $dN^{(5)}$ (Sec.~\ref{sec:targets}). Your BFKL formula is correct.
\end{description}
\paragraph{The split.} Every row is split exactly, term by term,
\begin{equation}
\frac{d^3N_{\rm row}}{d^3k}=\log\frac{k^+}{\Lambda}\;\mathfrak T_{\rm row}+\log\frac{\vee}{k^+}\;\mathfrak P_{\rm row}+R_{\rm row},
\label{eq:split}
\end{equation}
with $\log\frac{\vee}{\Lambda}=\log\frac{k^+}{\Lambda}+\log\frac{\vee}{k^+}$ and the identities of Sec.~\ref{sec:logs}. $R_{\rm row}$ (the remaining terms) is finite for $\vee\to\infty$ and $\Lambda\to0$, which fixes $\mathfrak T$ and $\mathfrak P$ uniquely. Each of the 79 pointwise-evaluable terms equals its three parts to $1.5\times10^{-14}$.
\paragraph{Results.}
\begin{itemize}
\item \textbf{Three $\rho$\textquotesingle s $\propto\log\frac{k^+}{\Lambda}$} (22 terms): \textbf{zero} after symmetrization, $7.7\times10^{-16}$ (Sec.~\ref{sec:three}).
\item \textbf{Three $\rho$\textquotesingle s $\propto\log\frac{\vee}{k^+}$} (35 terms): \textbf{zero}, $1.5\times10^{-16}$.
\item \textbf{Four $\rho$\textquotesingle s} (\texttt{4rho.tex}, with $\big[\log\frac{k^+}{\Lambda}+\log\frac{\vee}{k^+}\big]$): \textbf{zero} after symmetrization, $1.3\times10^{-16}$ (Sec.~\ref{sec:four}). Their three- and two-$\rho$ remainders enter both lists.
\item \textbf{Two $\rho$\textquotesingle s $\propto\log\frac{k^+}{\Lambda}$ $=$ JIMWLK$\otimes$LO}, exactly: 13 kernel structures, 154 independent colour structures, 0 differences (Sec.~\ref{sec:two}). It is your JIMWLK formula $\sum_{i=1}^5dN^{(i)}$ after the correction M2' (Sec.~\ref{sec:targets}).
\item \textbf{Two $\rho$\textquotesingle s $\propto\log\frac{\vee}{k^+}$ $=$ BFKL$\otimes$LO}, exactly: 9 kernel structures, 109 colour structures, 0 differences (Sec.~\ref{sec:bfkl}). It is your BFKL formula, Eq.~(LOBFKL), as you wrote it.
\end{itemize}
Therefore
\begin{equation}
\frac{d^3N_{\rm LL}}{d^3k}=\mathcal N\Big[\log\frac{k^+}{\Lambda}\;{\rm JIMWLK}\otimes{\rm LO}+\log\frac{\vee}{k^+}\;{\rm BFKL}\otimes{\rm LO}\Big]
=-\mathcal N\log\frac{\Lambda}{k^+}\;{\rm JIMWLK}\otimes{\rm LO}+\mathcal N\log\frac{\vee}{k^+}\;{\rm BFKL}\otimes{\rm LO}.
\label{eq:LL}
\end{equation}
In your formulas $\delta Y=\log\frac{k^+}{\Lambda}$ for JIMWLK (the target evolves from $y_T=\log\Lambda$ to the gluon) and $\delta Y=\log\frac{\vee}{k^+}$ for BFKL (the projectile evolves from the gluon to $y_P=\log\vee$). Written with $\log\frac{\Lambda}{k^+}$, the JIMWLK term has the coefficient $-{\rm JIMWLK}\otimes{\rm LO}$.

\paragraph{Where the BFKL terms are.} In the explicit logarithms of the rows, and, in Group III Rows III, V, VI and Group II Row IV, inside line 1 (the $p^+$, resp.\ $\xi$, integral). There three exact identities bring them out: the subtraction of the plus prescription gives $-f(0)\log\frac{\vee}{k^+}$, which cancels the $\log\frac{\vee}{k^+}$ of line 2 exactly (I2); the Fourier transform of the $y'$ integral at $y'=z$ gives the terms (B) (I3); the Fourier transform of $\log(y'-y)^2$ gives $H_4$ (I4). Nothing is expanded or dropped.

\section{How the logarithms are split: exact identities}\label{sec:logs}
\paragraph{(I1) Explicit logarithms.} A term carries at most one logarithmic factor. Your brackets:
\begin{align}
\left[\log{\left(\frac{k^{+}}{\Lambda}\right)}+\log{\left(\frac{\vee}{k^{+}}-1\right)}\right]&=\log\frac{k^+}{\Lambda}+\log\frac{\vee}{k^+}+\log\Big(1-\frac{k^+}{\vee}\Big),\notag\\
\left[\log{\left[\frac{\vee}{k^{+}}\right]}+\log{\left[\frac{k^{+}}{\Lambda}\right]}\right]&=\log\frac{k^+}{\Lambda}+\log\frac{\vee}{k^+}.\notag
\end{align}
All other logarithms ($A=(x-z)^2$ etc.; the remainder exactly and, where it depends on $\Lambda$, its limit):
\begin{align}
\log{\left(\frac{\vee}{k^{+}}-1\right)} &= \log\frac{\vee}{k^+} + \log{\left(1-\frac{k^{+}}{\vee}\right)} \notag\\
\log{\left(\frac{k^{+}-\Lambda}{\Lambda}\right)} &= \log\frac{k^+}{\Lambda} + \log{\left(1-\frac{\Lambda}{k^{+}}\right)}\notag\\&\qquad\xrightarrow{\Lambda\to0}\ \log\frac{k^+}{\Lambda} \notag\\
\log{\left[\frac{k^{+}-\Lambda}{\Lambda}\right]} &= \log\frac{k^+}{\Lambda} + \log{\left(1-\frac{\Lambda}{k^{+}}\right)}\notag\\&\qquad\xrightarrow{\Lambda\to0}\ \log\frac{k^+}{\Lambda} \notag\\
\log{\left[\frac{\Lambda}{k^{+}-\Lambda}\right]} &= -\log\frac{k^+}{\Lambda} - \log{\left(1-\frac{\Lambda}{k^{+}}\right)}\notag\\&\qquad\xrightarrow{\Lambda\to0}\ -\log\frac{k^+}{\Lambda} \notag\\
\log{\left[\frac{\vee}{\Lambda+k^{+}}\right]} &= \log\frac{\vee}{k^+} - \log{\left(1+\frac{\Lambda}{k^{+}}\right)}\notag\\&\qquad\xrightarrow{\Lambda\to0}\ \log\frac{\vee}{k^+} \notag\\
\log{\left[\frac{\vee}{k^{+}+\Lambda}\right]} &= \log\frac{\vee}{k^+} - \log{\left(1+\frac{\Lambda}{k^{+}}\right)}\notag\\&\qquad\xrightarrow{\Lambda\to0}\ \log\frac{\vee}{k^+} \notag\\
\log{\left[\frac{\left[\vee\, (x-z)^{2} - k^{+}\, (x-w)^{2}\right]}{\left[(\Lambda + k^{+})\, (x-z)^{2} - k^{+}\, (x-w)^{2}\right]}\right]} &= \log\frac{\vee}{k^+} + \log{\left[\frac{(x-z)^{2}-\frac{k^{+}}{\vee}\,(x-w)^{2}}{\left(1+\frac{\Lambda}{k^{+}}\right)(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}}\right]}\notag\\&\qquad\xrightarrow{\Lambda\to0}\ \log\frac{\vee}{k^+} + \log{\left[\frac{(x-z)^{2}-\frac{k^{+}}{\vee}\,(x-w)^{2}}{(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}}\right]} \notag\\
\log{\left[\frac{\left[(k^{+}-\Lambda)\, (x-z)^{2} - k^{+}\, (x-w)^{2}\right]}{\left[\Lambda\, (x-z)^{2} - k^{+}\, (x-w)^{2}\right]}\right]} &= \log{\left[\frac{(k^{+}-\Lambda)\,(x-z)^{2}-k^{+}\,(x-w)^{2}}{\Lambda\,(x-z)^{2}-k^{+}\,(x-w)^{2}}\right]}\notag\\&\qquad\xrightarrow{\Lambda\to0}\ \log{\left[\frac{(x-w)^{2}-(x-z)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}\right]} \notag\\
\log{\left[\frac{\left[k^{+}\,(x-w)^{2}-\vee\, (x-z)^{2}\right]}{\left[k^{+}\,(x-w)^{2}-\Lambda\, (x-z)^{2}\right]}\right]} &= \log\frac{\vee}{k^+} + \log{\left[\frac{\frac{k^{+}}{\vee}\,(x-w)^{2}-(x-z)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}-\frac{\Lambda}{k^{+}}\,(x-z)^{2}}\right]}\notag\\&\qquad\xrightarrow{\Lambda\to0}\ \log\frac{\vee}{k^+} + \log{\left[\frac{\frac{k^{+}}{\vee}\,(x-w)^{2}-(x-z)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}\right]} \notag\\
\log{\left[\frac{(x-w)^{2}\,k^{+}-(x-z)^{2}\,\vee}{(x-w)^{2}\,k^{+} - (x-z)^{2}\,(k^{+}+\Lambda)}\right]} &= \log\frac{\vee}{k^+} + \log{\left[\frac{(x-w)^{2}\,\frac{k^{+}}{\vee}-(x-z)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}-(x-z)^{2}\left(1+\frac{\Lambda}{k^{+}}\right)}\right]}\notag\\&\qquad\xrightarrow{\Lambda\to0}\ \log\frac{\vee}{k^+} + \log{\left[\frac{(x-w)^{2}\,\frac{k^{+}}{\vee}-(x-z)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}-(x-z)^{2}}\right]} \notag\\
\log{\left[\frac{(x-w)^{2}\,k^{+}-(x-z)^{2}\,(k^{+}-\Lambda)}{(x-w)^{2}\,k^{+}-(x-z)^{2}\,\Lambda}\right]} &= \log{\left[\frac{(x-w)^{2}\,k^{+}-(x-z)^{2}\,(k^{+}-\Lambda)}{(x-w)^{2}\,k^{+}-(x-z)^{2}\,\Lambda}\right]}\notag\\&\qquad\xrightarrow{\Lambda\to0}\ \log{\left[\frac{(x-w)^{2}-(x-z)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}\right]} \notag\\
\log{\left[\frac{\left[k^{+}\,(x-w)-(k^{+}-\Lambda)\,(x-z)\right]^{2}}{\left[k^{+}\,(x-w)-\Lambda\,(x-z)\right]^{2}}\right]} &= \log{\left[\frac{\left[k^{+}\,(x-w)-(k^{+}-\Lambda)\,(x-z)\right]^{2}}{\left[k^{+}\,(x-w)-\Lambda\,(x-z)\right]^{2}}\right]}\notag\\&\qquad\xrightarrow{\Lambda\to0}\ \log{\left[\frac{(z-w)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}\right]} \notag\\
\log{\left[\frac{(\vee-k^{+})\,(x-z)^{2}+k^{+}\,(x-w)^{2}}{\Lambda\, (x-z)^{2} + k^{+}\,(x-w)^{2}}\right]} &= \log\frac{\vee}{k^+} + \log{\left[\frac{\left(1-\frac{k^{+}}{\vee}\right)(x-z)^{2}+\frac{k^{+}}{\vee}\,(x-w)^{2}}{\frac{\Lambda}{k^{+}}\,(x-z)^{2}+(x-w)^{2}}\right]}\notag\\&\qquad\xrightarrow{\Lambda\to0}\ \log\frac{\vee}{k^+} + \log{\left[\frac{\left(1-\frac{k^{+}}{\vee}\right)(x-z)^{2}+\frac{k^{+}}{\vee}\,(x-w)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}\right]} \notag\\
\log{\left[\frac{(\vee-k^{+})\,(x-z)^{2}+k^{+}\,(x-w)^{2}}{k^{+}\,(x-w)^{2}+\Lambda\, (x-z)^{2}}\right]} &= \log\frac{\vee}{k^+} + \log{\left[\frac{\left(1-\frac{k^{+}}{\vee}\right)(x-z)^{2}+\frac{k^{+}}{\vee}\,(x-w)^{2}}{\frac{\Lambda}{k^{+}}\,(x-z)^{2}+(x-w)^{2}}\right]}\notag\\&\qquad\xrightarrow{\Lambda\to0}\ \log\frac{\vee}{k^+} + \log{\left[\frac{\left(1-\frac{k^{+}}{\vee}\right)(x-z)^{2}+\frac{k^{+}}{\vee}\,(x-w)^{2}}{(x-w)^{2}}\right]} \notag\\
\log{\left[\frac{(\vee-k^{+})\,(x-z)^{2}+k^{+}\,(x-w')^{2}}{\Lambda\, (x-z)^{2} + k^{+}\,(x-w')^{2}}\right]} &= \log\frac{\vee}{k^+} + \log{\left[\frac{\left(1-\frac{k^{+}}{\vee}\right)(x-z)^{2}+\frac{k^{+}}{\vee}\,(x-w')^{2}}{\frac{\Lambda}{k^{+}}\,(x-z)^{2}+(x-w')^{2}}\right]}\notag\\&\qquad\xrightarrow{\Lambda\to0}\ \log\frac{\vee}{k^+} + \log{\left[\frac{\left(1-\frac{k^{+}}{\vee}\right)(x-z)^{2}+\frac{k^{+}}{\vee}\,(x-w')^{2}}{(x-w')^{2}}\right]} \notag\\
\log{\left[\frac{(\vee-k^{+})\,(x'-z)^{2}+k^{+}\,(x'-w')^{2}}{\Lambda\,(x'-z)^{2}+k^{+}\,(x'-w')^{2}}\right]} &= \log\frac{\vee}{k^+} + \log{\left[\frac{\left(1-\frac{k^{+}}{\vee}\right)(x'-z)^{2}+\frac{k^{+}}{\vee}\,(x'-w')^{2}}{\frac{\Lambda}{k^{+}}\,(x'-z)^{2}+(x'-w')^{2}}\right]}\notag\\&\qquad\xrightarrow{\Lambda\to0}\ \log\frac{\vee}{k^+} + \log{\left[\frac{\left(1-\frac{k^{+}}{\vee}\right)(x'-z)^{2}+\frac{k^{+}}{\vee}\,(x'-w')^{2}}{(x'-w')^{2}}\right]} \notag\\
\log{\left[\frac{k^{+}\, (x'-w')^{2} + (\vee - k^{+})\, (x'-z)^{2}}{k^{+}\, (x'-w')^{2} + \Lambda\, (x'-z)^{2}}\right]} &= \log\frac{\vee}{k^+} + \log{\left[\frac{\left(1-\frac{k^{+}}{\vee}\right)(x'-z)^{2}+\frac{k^{+}}{\vee}\,(x'-w')^{2}}{\frac{\Lambda}{k^{+}}\,(x'-z)^{2}+(x'-w')^{2}}\right]}\notag\\&\qquad\xrightarrow{\Lambda\to0}\ \log\frac{\vee}{k^+} + \log{\left[\frac{\left(1-\frac{k^{+}}{\vee}\right)(x'-z)^{2}+\frac{k^{+}}{\vee}\,(x'-w')^{2}}{(x'-w')^{2}}\right]} \notag\\
\log{\left[\frac{(\vee-k^{+})\,(x-z)^{2}+k^{+}\,(x-w)^{2}}{k^{+}\, (x'-w')^{2} + (\vee-k^{+})\, (x'-z)^{2}}\right]} &= \log{\left[\frac{\left(1-\frac{k^{+}}{\vee}\right)(x-z)^{2}+\frac{k^{+}}{\vee}\,(x-w)^{2}}{\frac{k^{+}}{\vee}\, (x'-w')^{2} + \left(1-\frac{k^{+}}{\vee}\right) (x'-z)^{2}}\right]} \notag\\
\log{\left[\frac{\Lambda\,(x-z)^{2}+k^{+}\,(x-w)^{2}}{k^{+}\, (x'-w')^{2} + \Lambda\, (x'-z)^{2}}\right]} &= \log{\left[\frac{\Lambda\,(x-z)^{2}+k^{+}\,(x-w)^{2}}{k^{+}\, (x'-w')^{2} + \Lambda\, (x'-z)^{2}}\right]}\notag\\&\qquad\xrightarrow{\Lambda\to0}\ \log{\left[\frac{(x-w)^{2}}{(x'-w')^{2}}\right]}
\end{align}

Logarithms of $\epsilon$, $\mu$, $\log(1-\frac{k^+}{\vee})$ and $\log(1-\frac{\Lambda}{k^+})$ are finite and are not split. Three special cases:
\begin{itemize}
\item Group I Row II part 1: $\big[\frac{11}{3}-2\log\frac{k^+}{\Lambda}+\log(1-\frac{k^+}{\vee})\big]$ times the pole bracket; only $-2\log\frac{k^+}{\Lambda}$ is large.
\item Group I Row V 2b UV: $\big[2\log\frac{k^+}{\Lambda}+2\log(1-\frac{\Lambda}{k^+})-\frac{11}{6}\big]$.
\item Group I Row V 2b finite, line 6: $\big[-\frac{11}{6}+2\log\frac{k^+-\Lambda}{\Lambda}\big]$.
\end{itemize}

\paragraph{(I2) The subtraction of the plus prescription.}\label{sec:exact} With your $\left[\frac{1}{p^+}\right]_+f=\frac{f(p^+)-f(0)}{p^+}$, define $\left[\frac{k^+}{p^+(p^++k^+)}\right]_+f\equiv\frac{k^+[f(p^+)-f(0)]}{p^+(p^++k^+)}$. Since $\frac{1}{p^+}=\frac{1}{p^++k^+}+\frac{k^+}{p^+(p^++k^+)}$ and $\int_0^{\vee-k^+}\frac{dp^+}{p^++k^+}=\log\frac{\vee}{k^+}$,
\begin{equation}
\int_0^{\vee-k^+}dp^+\left[\frac{1}{p^+}\right]_+f(p^+)=\int_0^{\vee-k^+}dp^+\left(\left[\frac{k^+}{p^+(p^++k^+)}\right]_++\frac{1}{p^++k^+}\right)f(p^+)-f(0)\log\frac{\vee}{k^+}.
\label{eq:I2}
\end{equation}
For $\xi$, with your $\frac{d\xi}{\xi^2}\frac{\xi}{(1-\xi)_+}g=\frac{d\xi}{\xi(1-\xi)}[g(\xi)-g(1)]$, define $\frac{d\xi}{\xi^2}\frac{\xi^2}{(1-\xi)_+}g\equiv\frac{d\xi}{1-\xi}[g(\xi)-g(1)]$. Then $\frac{1}{\xi(1-\xi)}=\frac{1}{\xi}+\frac{1}{1-\xi}$ gives
\begin{equation}
\int_{\frac{k^+}{\vee}}^1\frac{d\xi}{\xi^2}\frac{\xi}{(1-\xi)_+}g=\int_{\frac{k^+}{\vee}}^1\frac{d\xi}{\xi^2}\Big(\xi+\frac{\xi^2}{(1-\xi)_+}\Big)g-g(1)\log\frac{\vee}{k^+}.
\label{eq:I2xi}
\end{equation}
Your line 2 is $f(0)\big[\log\frac{k^+}{\Lambda}+\log(\frac{\vee}{k^+}-1)\big]$ (resp.\ $g(1)[\dots]$): it came from this subtraction. So its $\log\frac{\vee}{k^+}$, (i), and $-f(0)\log\frac{\vee}{k^+}$, (ii), cancel exactly, poles included; $\log\frac{k^+}{\Lambda}$ (JIMWLK) and $\log(1-\frac{k^+}{\vee})$ (remaining) are left.

\paragraph{(I3) The $y'$ integral at $y'=z$.} In line 1 of Group III Rows III(1), V, VI take the $-\frac{1}{k^+}\delta_{im}\delta_{jk'}$ term; in Row III(2) the $-\frac{p^+}{k^{+2}}$ term. Its coefficient tends to a constant at large $p^+$. Write
\begin{equation*}
G^{k'}(y')=\frac{(x'-y')^{k'}}{(x'-y')^2}\times\big[\text{colour bracket with }U(y')\big]=G^{k'}(z)+\big[G^{k'}(y')-G^{k'}(z)\big].
\end{equation*}
With $\int_u e^{-iq\cdot u}\frac{u^m}{u^2}=-2\pi i\frac{q^m}{q^2}$ and $q=k\frac{p^++k^+}{k^+}$, exactly,
\begin{equation}
\int_{y'}e^{-ik\cdot(y'-w)}e^{-ik\cdot\frac{p^+}{k^+}(y'-z)}\frac{(y'-z)^m}{(y'-z)^2}\,G^{k'}(z)=\frac{k^+}{p^++k^+}\int_{w'}e^{-ik\cdot(w'-w)}\frac{(w'-z)^m}{(w'-z)^2}\,G^{k'}(z).
\label{eq:I3}
\end{equation}
For Rows III(1), V, VI: $\int_0^{\vee-k^+}\frac{dp^+}{2\pi}\big(-\frac{1}{k^+}\big)\frac{k^+}{p^++k^+}=-\frac{1}{2\pi}\log\frac{\vee}{k^+}$, exactly. This is (B), (iii), with nothing left over. The $G(y')-G(z)$ part stays in the remaining terms.

\paragraph{(I5) Row III(2).} With $A=(x-z)^2$, $B=(x-w)^2$,
\begin{equation}
-\frac{p^+}{k^{+2}}\,\frac{k^+}{p^++k^+}\,\frac{1}{p^+A+k^+B}=-\frac{1}{k^+A\,(p^++k^+)}+\frac{B}{A\,(p^++k^+)(p^+A+k^+B)}.
\label{eq:I5}
\end{equation}
The first term gives (B). The second falls like $\frac{1}{p^{+2}}$ and stays in the remaining terms.

\paragraph{(I4) Group II Row IV.} In the $\frac{1-\xi}{\xi}$ term of line 1 write $H(y,y')=H(y,y)+[H(y,y')-H(y,y)]$, $H(y,y')=\frac{(x-y)\cdot(x'-y')}{(x-y)^2(x'-y')^2}[U^{e'c}(y')-U^{e'c}(x')][U^{ec}(y)-U^{ce}(x)]\rho^{e'}(x')\rho^e(x)$. The constant part of the pole bracket gives $\delta^{(2)}(k/\xi)=0$ for $k\neq0$, and $\int_{y'}e^{-ik\cdot(y'-y)\frac{1}{\xi}}\log\frac{(y'-y)^2\mu^2}{4}=-\frac{4\pi\xi^2}{k^2}$. Also $\int_{\frac{k^+}{\vee}}^1d\xi\,\frac{1-\xi}{\xi}=\log\frac{\vee}{k^+}-1+\frac{k^+}{\vee}$ and $\int_{w,w'}e^{-ik\cdot(w'-w)}\mathcal K(w-z)\cdot\mathcal K(w'-z)=\frac{4\pi^2}{k^2}$. So the $H(y,y)$ part is, exactly,
\begin{equation}
\frac{g^2}{4\pi^2}\frac{N_c}{(2\pi)^3}\frac{g^2}{2\pi^2}\frac{1}{k^+}\,\frac{d_\perp}{2}\,\frac{4\pi}{k^2}\Big[\log\frac{\vee}{k^+}-1+\frac{k^+}{\vee}\Big]\int_{x,x',z}H(z,z)
=\Big[\log\frac{\vee}{k^+}-1+\frac{k^+}{\vee}\Big]\,H_4\Big|_{\log\frac{\vee}{k^+}\to1},
\label{eq:I4}
\end{equation}
at $d_\perp=2$ (no pole multiplies it). Its $\log\frac{\vee}{k^+}$ term is $\log\frac{\vee}{k^+}H_4$, (iii) of Group II Row IV.

\paragraph{The split is complete.} After these steps every remaining $p^+$ ($\xi$) integrand falls at least like $\frac{1}{p^{+2}}$ after the transverse integrals ($G(y')-G(z)$ and $H(y,y')-H(y,y)$ vanish at the coincident point; the $\frac{1}{p^++k^+}$ and $\left[\frac{k^+}{p^+(p^++k^+)}\right]_+$ terms carry the $\frac{k^+}{p^++k^+}$ of (I3)). So the remaining terms stay finite for $\vee\to\infty$, and the coefficient of $\log\frac{\vee}{k^+}$ is unique; $\vee$ stays exact in the remaining terms.

\paragraph{Numerical checks} (\texttt{bfkl\_rows/exact\_check.py}; two configurations, $\vee=20k^+$, the $p^+$ and $\xi$ integrals done numerically, pointwise in the transverse points; line 1 of these rows is the same in your new files):
\begin{itemize}
\item line 1 $=$ (rewritten line 1) $+$ ($G(y')-G(z)$ term) $+$ ($-f(0)\log\frac{\vee}{k^+}$) $+$ ($G(z)$ term), Group III Rows III(1), III(2), V, VI; line 1 $=$ (rewritten line 1) $+$ ($-g(1)\log\frac{\vee}{k^+}$), Group II Row IV: $\le3.7\times10^{-15}$;
\item the $G(z)$ term in the form \eqref{eq:I3}, $p^+$ integral done, $=$ (B) ($+$ the second term of \eqref{eq:I5} in Row III(2)): $\le3.4\times10^{-15}$;
\item Group II Row IV: (i) $+$ (ii) $=0$: $5.5\times10^{-16}$;
\item the Fourier transforms: $q\int_0^\infty J_1(qr)\,e^{-ar}dr$ at $a=10^{-2},3\times10^{-3},10^{-3}$ (must tend to 1): $q=0.7$: 0.9857, 0.9957, 0.9986; $q=1.9$: 0.9947, 0.9984, 0.9995. $2\pi\int_0^\infty rJ_0(qr)\log r^2\,e^{-ar^2}dr\big/(-\frac{4\pi}{q^2})$ at $a=4,2,1\times10^{-3}$, extrapolated to $a=0$ (must be 1): $q=0.7$: 1.0350, 1.0169, 1.0083 $\to$ 0.99971; $q=1.9$: 1.0045, 1.0022, 1.0011 $\to$ 1.00000.
\end{itemize}

\section{Group I (with c.c.)}\label{sec:LLgI}
\subsection{Row I}
Only four $\rho$\textquotesingle s: Sec.~\ref{sec:four} (with the same coefficient for both logarithms).
\subsection{Row II}
\paragraph{Row II, first part.}
Your bracket $\big[\frac{11}{3}-2\log\frac{k^+}{\Lambda}+\log(1-\frac{k^+}{\vee})\big]$ multiplies the pole bracket; only its $-2\log\frac{k^+}{\Lambda}$ is a large logarithm. The term $\log(1-\frac{k^+}{\vee})\log\frac{\vee}{k^+}$ is not a BFKL term: its coefficient depends on $\vee$, it is bounded for every $\vee>k^+$ ($0\le-\log(1-\frac{k^+}{\vee})\log\frac{\vee}{k^+}\le\log^22$, maximum at $\vee=2k^+$) and vanishes for $\vee\to\infty$; it stays with the remaining terms.
\noindent Terms $\propto\log\frac{k^+}{\Lambda}$:
\begin{align}
     \frac{d^{3}N_{1\,\rm NLO}^{\rm}}{d^{3}k} &  =  \frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\, \frac{g^{4}\,N_{c}}{32\pi^{4}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\, \int_{w, w',x,x',} e^{-ik(w^\prime-w)}\, \frac{(x'-w')\cdot (x-w)}{(x'-w')^{2}\, (x-w)^{2}}\, \Bigg[\left[-\frac{2}{\epsilon}-2\gamma-\log{\left[\frac{(x-w)^{2}\,\mu^{2}_{\overline{MS}}}{4}\right]}\right]\times \notag \\ & \left(-2\,\log{\left(\frac{k^{+}}{\Lambda}\right)}\right)\Bigg]\times\notag \\ & \left[U^{ab'}(x')\,\rho^{b'}(x') - U^{ab'}(w')\,\rho^{b'}(x')\right]\times\,\left[U^{ab}(w)\,\rho^{b}(x) - U^{ab}(x)\,\rho^{b}(x)\right] \label{eq:J:G1II1}
\end{align}

\noindent Terms $\propto\log\frac{\vee}{k^+}$: none.

\paragraph{Row II, second part, $d^3N_{2a}$.}
Line 1 has $\big[\log\frac{\vee}{k^+}+\log\frac{k^+}{\Lambda}\big]$ and enters both lists. In lines 2 and 3 the $z$ integral is already done (hence $\frac{1}{32\pi^4}$); the measure should read $\int_{x,y,x'}$.
\noindent Terms $\propto\log\frac{k^+}{\Lambda}$:
\begin{align}
    \frac{d^{3}N_{2\,a}}{d^{3}k} & =  -\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{i\,g^{4}\,f^{hbc}}{32\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\log{\left(\frac{k^{+}}{\Lambda}\right)}\,
\int_{w,w'} e^{-ik\cdot(w'-w)}\int_{x,y,x',z}\frac{(x'-w')^{i}}{(x'-w')^{2}}\,\Bigg[\frac{(x-w)^{i}}{(x-w)^{2}}\, \frac{(w-z)\cdot (y-z)}{(w-z)^{2}\,(y-z)^{2}}\notag \\ & - \frac{(y-w)^{i}}{(y-w)^{2}}\, \frac{(w-z)\cdot (x-z)}{(w-z)^{2}\,(x-z)^{2}}\Bigg]\times\Big[U^{ab'}(x')\,\rho^{b'}(x')-U^{ab'}(w')\,\rho^{b'}(x')\Big]\times\, \Big[U^{ah}(w)\,\{\rho^{b}(x),\rho^{c}(y)\}\Big] \notag \\
& -\,\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{i\,g^{4}\,f^{abc}}{32\pi^{4}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\log{\left(\frac{k^{+}}{\Lambda}\right)}\,
\int_{w,w'} e^{-ik\cdot(w'-w)}\int_{x,y,x',z}\frac{(x'-w')^{i}}{(x'-w')^{2}}\,\Bigg[\frac{(x-w)^{i}}{(x-w)^{2}}\,\log\frac{(w-y)^{2}}{(x-y)^{2}}\notag \\ & -\frac{(y-w)^{i}}{(y-w)^{2}}\,\log\frac{(w-x)^{2}}{(x-y)^{2}}\Bigg]\times \Big[U^{ab'}(x')\,\rho^{b'}(x')-U^{ab'}(w')\,\rho^{b'}(x')\Big]\times\, \Big[U^{bd}(x)\,U^{ce}(y)\,\{\rho^{d}(x),\rho^{e}(y)\}\Big] \label{eq:J:G1II2a}
\end{align}

\noindent Terms $\propto\log\frac{\vee}{k^+}$:
\begin{align}
    \frac{d^{3}N_{2\,a}}{d^{3}k} & =  -\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{i\,g^{4}\,f^{hbc}}{32\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\log{\left(\frac{\vee}{k^{+}}\right)}\,
\int_{w,w'} e^{-ik\cdot(w'-w)}\int_{x,y,x',z}\frac{(x'-w')^{i}}{(x'-w')^{2}}\,\Bigg[\frac{(x-w)^{i}}{(x-w)^{2}}\, \frac{(w-z)\cdot (y-z)}{(w-z)^{2}\,(y-z)^{2}}\notag \\ & - \frac{(y-w)^{i}}{(y-w)^{2}}\, \frac{(w-z)\cdot (x-z)}{(w-z)^{2}\,(x-z)^{2}}\Bigg]\times\Big[U^{ab'}(x')\,\rho^{b'}(x')-U^{ab'}(w')\,\rho^{b'}(x')\Big]\times\, \Big[U^{ah}(w)\,\{\rho^{b}(x),\rho^{c}(y)\}\Big] \notag \\
& -\,\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{i\,g^{4}\,f^{hbc}}{32\pi^{4}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\log{\left(\frac{\vee}{k^{+}}\right)}\,
\int_{w,w'} e^{-ik\cdot(w'-w)}\int_{x,y,x',z}\frac{(x'-w')^{i}}{(x'-w')^{2}}\,\Bigg[\frac{(x-w)^{i}}{(x-w)^{2}}\,\log\frac{(w-y)^{2}}{(x-y)^{2}}-\frac{(y-w)^{i}}{(y-w)^{2}}\,\log\frac{(w-x)^{2}}{(x-y)^{2}}\Bigg]\times\notag \\ & \Big[U^{ab'}(x')\,\rho^{b'}(x')-U^{ab'}(w')\,\rho^{b'}(x')\Big]\times\, \Big[U^{ah}(w)\,\{\rho^{b}(x),\rho^{c}(y)\}\Big] \label{eq:B:G1II2a}
\end{align}

\paragraph{Row II, second part, $d^3N_{2b}$.}
No large logarithm ($R^i$ depends on $\vee$ only through $\frac{k^+}{\vee}$).
\noindent Terms $\propto\log\frac{k^+}{\Lambda}$: none.

\noindent Terms $\propto\log\frac{\vee}{k^+}$: none.

\subsection{Row III}
\paragraph{Row III, $d^3N_1$.}
Lines 1 and 4 (your green $\frac{1}{\Lambda}$ brackets) are not logarithms. Together with line 1 of $d^3N_2$ and line 1 of $d^3N_{3a}$ they cancel exactly (\texttt{Remaining\_RC\_DGLAP\_finite.pdf}).
\noindent Terms $\propto\log\frac{k^+}{\Lambda}$:
\begin{align}
   \frac{d^{3}N_{1\,\rm NLO}^{\rm}}{d^{3}k}  & =  \frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}  \int_{w, w^\prime} e^{-ik(w^\prime-w)}\, \Bigg\{\notag \\
& -\,\frac{ig^{4}\,f^{abd}}{16\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\log{\left(\frac{k^{+}}{\Lambda}\right)}\,\int_{x,y,z,x'}\, \frac{(x'-w')\cdot (y-z)}{(x'-w')^{2}\,(y-z)^{2}}\,\frac{\left[(x-z)^{2}+(x-w)^{2}\right]}{\left[(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\right]^{2}}\notag \\ & \left[U^{ab'}(x')\,\rho^{b'}(x')-U^{ab'}(w')\, \rho^{b'}(x')\right]\times\, \left[U^{de}(x)\,U^{bc}(z)\, \rho^{e}(x)\,\rho^{c}(y)-U^{de}(x)\,U^{bc}(y)\,\rho^{e}(x)\,\rho^{c}(y)\right] \notag \\
& -\,\frac{ig^{4}\,f^{abd}}{16\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\left(-\log{\left(\frac{k^{+}}{\Lambda}\right)}\right)\,\int_{x,y,z,x'}\, \frac{(x'-w')\cdot (y-z)}{(x'-w')^{2}\,(y-z)^{2}}\,\frac{\left[(x-z)^{2}+(x-w)^{2}\right]}{\left[(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\right]^{2}}\notag \\ & \left[U^{ab'}(x')\,\rho^{b'}(x')-U^{ab'}(w')\, \rho^{b'}(x')\right]\times\, \left[U^{de}(x)\,U^{bc}(z)\, \rho^{e}(x)\,\rho^{c}(y)-U^{de}(x)\,U^{bc}(y)\,\rho^{e}(x)\,\rho^{c}(y)\right] \notag \\
& +\, \frac{ig^{4}\,f^{abd}}{16\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\log{\left(\frac{k^{+}}{\Lambda}\right)}\,\int_{x,y,z,x'} \frac{(x'-w')\cdot (x-w)\, (x-z)\cdot (y-z)}{(x'-w')^{2}\, (x-w)^{2}\, (x-z)^{2}\, (y-z)^{2}}\, \notag \\ & \left[U^{ab'}(x')\,\rho^{b'}(x')-U^{ab'}(w')\, \rho^{b'}(x')\right]\times\, \left[U^{de}(x)\,U^{bc}(z)\, \rho^{e}(x)\,\rho^{c}(y)-U^{de}(x)\,U^{bc}(y)\,\rho^{e}(x)\,\rho^{c}(y)\right]\Bigg\} \label{eq:J:G1IIIN1}
\end{align}

\noindent Terms $\propto\log\frac{\vee}{k^+}$:
