   \frac{d^{3}N_{1\,\rm NLO}^{\rm}}{d^{3}k}  & =  \frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}  \int_{w, w^\prime} e^{-ik(w^\prime-w)}\, \Bigg\{\notag \\
& -\,\frac{ig^{4}\,f^{abd}}{16\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\log{\left(\frac{\vee}{k^{+}}\right)}\,\int_{x,y,z,x'}\, \frac{(x'-w')\cdot (y-z)}{(x'-w')^{2}\,(y-z)^{2}}\,\frac{\left[(x-z)^{2}+(x-w)^{2}\right]}{\left[(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\right]^{2}}\notag \\ & \left[U^{ab'}(x')\,\rho^{b'}(x')-U^{ab'}(w')\, \rho^{b'}(x')\right]\times\, \left[U^{de}(x)\,U^{bc}(z)\, \rho^{e}(x)\,\rho^{c}(y)-U^{de}(x)\,U^{bc}(y)\,\rho^{e}(x)\,\rho^{c}(y)\right] \notag \\
&  +\frac{ig^{4}\,f^{abd}}{16\pi^{5}}\, \frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\int_{x,y,z,x'}\, \frac{(x'-w')\cdot (y-z)}{(x'-w')^{2}\,(y-z)^{2}}\, \frac{\left[(x-w)^{2}+(x-z)^{2}\right]}{\left[(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\right]^{2}}\, \times\, \log{\left(\frac{\vee}{k^{+}}\right)}\notag \\ & \left[U^{ab'}(x')\,\rho^{b'}(x')-U^{ab'}(w')\, \rho^{b'}(x')\right]\times\, \left[U^{de}(x)\,U^{bc}(z)\, \rho^{e}(x)\,\rho^{c}(y)-U^{de}(x)\,U^{bc}(y)\,\rho^{e}(x)\,\rho^{c}(y)\right] \notag \\
& +\, \frac{ig^{4}\,f^{abd}}{16\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\log{\left(\frac{\vee}{k^{+}}\right)}\,\int_{x,y,z,x'} \frac{(x'-w')\cdot (x-w)\, (x-z)\cdot (y-z)}{(x'-w')^{2}\, (x-w)^{2}\, (x-z)^{2}\, (y-z)^{2}}\, \notag \\ & \left[U^{ab'}(x')\,\rho^{b'}(x')-U^{ab'}(w')\, \rho^{b'}(x')\right]\times\, \left[U^{de}(x)\,U^{bc}(z)\, \rho^{e}(x)\,\rho^{c}(y)-U^{de}(x)\,U^{bc}(y)\,\rho^{e}(x)\,\rho^{c}(y)\right] \notag \\
&-\, \frac{ig^{4}\,f^{abd}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\int_{x,y,z,x'}\frac{(x'-w')\cdot (x-w)\, (x-z)\cdot (y-z)}{(x'-w')^{2}\, (x-w)^{2}\, (x-z)^{2}\, (y-z)^{2}}\,\log{\left(\frac{\vee}{k^{+}}\right)} \notag \\ & \left[U^{ab'}(x')\,\rho^{b'}(x')-U^{ab'}(w')\, \rho^{b'}(x')\right]\times\, \left[U^{de}(x)\,U^{bc}(z)\, \rho^{e}(x)\,\rho^{c}(y)-U^{de}(x)\,U^{bc}(y)\,\rho^{e}(x)\,\rho^{c}(y)\right]\Bigg\} \label{eq:B:G1IIIN1}
\end{align}

\paragraph{Row III, $d^3N_2$.}
Line 1 is a $\frac{1}{\Lambda}$ term (see $d^3N_1$). Your two red lines cancel each other and are left out.
\noindent Terms $\propto\log\frac{k^+}{\Lambda}$:
\begin{align}
   \frac{d^{3}N_{2\,\rm NLO}^{\rm}}{d^{3}k}  & =  -\,\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}  \int_{w, w^\prime} e^{-ik(w^\prime-w)}\, \Bigg\{\notag \\
&  + \frac{ig^{4}\, f^{abd}}{8\pi^{5}}\, \frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\log{\left(\frac{k^{+}}{\Lambda}\right)}\, \int_{x,y,z,x'}\,\frac{(x'-w')\cdot  (y-z)\, (z-w)\cdot (x-z)\, (x-w)^{2}}{(x'-w')^{2}\, (y-z)^{2}\, (z-w)^{2}\,\left[(x-w)^{2}-(x-z)^{2}\right]^{2}}\, \notag \\ & \left[U^{ab'}(x')\,\rho^{b'}(x')-U^{ab'}(w')\, \rho^{b'}(x')\right]\times\,\left[U^{de}(x)\,U^{bc}(z)\, \rho^{e}(x)\,\rho^{c}(y)-U^{de}(x)\,U^{bc}(y)\,\rho^{e}(x)\,\rho^{c}(y)\right] \notag \\
&   - \frac{ig^{4}\, f^{abd}}{8\pi^{5}}\, \frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\log{\left(\frac{k^{+}}{\Lambda}\right)}\, \int_{x,y,z,x'}\,\frac{(x'-w')\cdot  (y-z)\, (z-w)\cdot (x-w)\, (x-z)^{2}}{(x'-w')^{2}\, (y-z)^{2}\, (z-w)^{2}\,\left[(x-w)^{2}-(x-z)^{2}\right]^{2}}\, \notag \\ & \left[U^{ab'}(x')\,\rho^{b'}(x')-U^{ab'}(w')\, \rho^{b'}(x')\right]\times\,\left[U^{de}(x)\,U^{bc}(z)\, \rho^{e}(x)\,\rho^{c}(y)-U^{de}(x)\,U^{bc}(y)\,\rho^{e}(x)\,\rho^{c}(y)\right]\Bigg\} \label{eq:J:G1IIIN2}
\end{align}

\noindent Terms $\propto\log\frac{\vee}{k^+}$:
\begin{align}
   \frac{d^{3}N_{2\,\rm NLO}^{\rm}}{d^{3}k}  & =  -\,\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}  \int_{w, w^\prime} e^{-ik(w^\prime-w)}\, \Bigg\{\notag \\
& -\frac{ig^{4}\, f^{abd}}{8\pi^{5}}\, \frac{1}{k^{+}}\, \log{\left(\frac{\vee}{k^{+}}\right)}\,\int_{x,y,z,x'}\,\frac{(x'-w')\cdot (x-w)\, (y-z)\cdot (z-w)}{(x'-w')^{2}\, (y-z)^{2}\, (z-w)^{2}\,(x-w)^{2}}\, \notag \\ & \left[U^{ab'}(x')\,\rho^{b'}(x')-U^{ab'}(w')\, \rho^{b'}(x')\right]\times\,\left[U^{de}(x)\,U^{bc}(z)\, \rho^{e}(x)\,\rho^{c}(y)-U^{de}(x)\,U^{bc}(y)\,\rho^{e}(x)\,\rho^{c}(y)\right] \notag \\
&  + \frac{ig^{4}\, f^{abd}}{8\pi^{5}}\, \frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\log{\left(\frac{\vee}{k^{+}}\right)}\, \int_{x,y,z,x'}\,\frac{(x'-w')\cdot  (y-z)\, (z-w)\cdot (x-z)\, (x-w)^{2}}{(x'-w')^{2}\, (y-z)^{2}\, (z-w)^{2}\,\left[(x-w)^{2}-(x-z)^{2}\right]^{2}}\, \notag \\ & \left[U^{ab'}(x')\,\rho^{b'}(x')-U^{ab'}(w')\, \rho^{b'}(x')\right]\times\,\left[U^{de}(x)\,U^{bc}(z)\, \rho^{e}(x)\,\rho^{c}(y)-U^{de}(x)\,U^{bc}(y)\,\rho^{e}(x)\,\rho^{c}(y)\right] \notag \\
&   - \frac{ig^{4}\, f^{abd}}{8\pi^{5}}\, \frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\log{\left(\frac{\vee}{k^{+}}\right)}\, \int_{x,y,z,x'}\,\frac{(x'-w')\cdot  (y-z)\, (z-w)\cdot (x-w)\, (x-z)^{2}}{(x'-w')^{2}\, (y-z)^{2}\, (z-w)^{2}\,\left[(x-w)^{2}-(x-z)^{2}\right]^{2}}\, \notag \\ & \left[U^{ab'}(x')\,\rho^{b'}(x')-U^{ab'}(w')\, \rho^{b'}(x')\right]\times\,\left[U^{de}(x)\,U^{bc}(z)\, \rho^{e}(x)\,\rho^{c}(y)-U^{de}(x)\,U^{bc}(y)\,\rho^{e}(x)\,\rho^{c}(y)\right] \notag \\
&  + \frac{ig^{4}\, f^{abd}}{8\pi^{5}}\, \frac{1}{k^{+}}\, \int_{x,y,z,x'}\,\log{\left(\frac{\vee}{k^{+}}\right)}\,\frac{(x'-w')\cdot (z-w)\, (y-z)\cdot (x-w)}{(x'-w')^{2}\, (y-z)^{2}\, (z-w)^{2}\,\left[(x-w)^{2}-(x-z)^{2}\right]}\, \notag \\ & \left[U^{ab'}(x')\,\rho^{b'}(x')-U^{ab'}(w')\, \rho^{b'}(x')\right]\times\,\left[U^{de}(x)\,U^{bc}(z)\, \rho^{e}(x)\,\rho^{c}(y)-U^{de}(x)\,U^{bc}(y)\,\rho^{e}(x)\,\rho^{c}(y)\right] \notag \\
& -\,\frac{ig^{4}\, f^{abd}}{8\pi^{5}}\, \frac{1}{k^{+}}\, \int_{x,y,z,x'}\,\log{\left(\frac{\vee}{k^{+}}\right)}\,\frac{(x'-w')\cdot (x-w)\, (y-z)\cdot(z-w)\,(x-z)^{2}}{(x'-w')^{2}\, (y-z)^{2}\, (z-w)^{2}\,\left[(x-w)^{2}-(x-z)^{2}\right]\,(x-w)^{2}}\, \notag \\ & \left[U^{ab'}(x')\,\rho^{b'}(x')-U^{ab'}(w')\, \rho^{b'}(x')\right]\times\,\left[U^{de}(x)\,U^{bc}(z)\, \rho^{e}(x)\,\rho^{c}(y)-U^{de}(x)\,U^{bc}(y)\,\rho^{e}(x)\,\rho^{c}(y)\right] \notag \\
& +\frac{ig^{4}\, f^{abd}}{8\pi^{5}}\, \frac{1}{k^{+}}\, \int_{x,y,z,x'}\,\log{\left(\frac{\vee}{k^{+}}\right)}\,\frac{(x'-w')\cdot  (y-z)\, (z-w)\cdot (x-w)\, (x-z)^{2}}{(x'-w')^{2}\, (y-z)^{2}\, (z-w)^{2}\,\left[(x-w)^{2}-(x-z)^{2}\right]^{2}}\, \notag \\ & \left[U^{ab'}(x')\,\rho^{b'}(x')-U^{ab'}(w')\, \rho^{b'}(x')\right]\times\,\left[U^{de}(x)\,U^{bc}(z)\, \rho^{e}(x)\,\rho^{c}(y)-U^{de}(x)\,U^{bc}(y)\,\rho^{e}(x)\,\rho^{c}(y)\right] \notag \\
& - \frac{ig^{4}\, f^{abd}}{8\pi^{5}}\, \frac{1}{k^{+}}\, \int_{x,y,z,x'}\,\log{\left(\frac{\vee}{k^{+}}\right)}\,\frac{(x'-w')\cdot (z-w)\, (y-z)\cdot (x-z)\, (x-w)^{2}}{(x'-w')^{2}\, (y-z)^{2}\, (z-w)^{2}\,\left[(x-w)^{2} - (x-z)^{2}\right]\, (x-z)^{2}}\, \notag \\ & \left[U^{ab'}(x')\,\rho^{b'}(x')-U^{ab'}(w')\, \rho^{b'}(x')\right]\times\,\left[U^{de}(x)\,U^{bc}(z)\, \rho^{e}(x)\,\rho^{c}(y)-U^{de}(x)\,U^{bc}(y)\,\rho^{e}(x)\,\rho^{c}(y)\right] \notag \\
& +\frac{ig^{4}\, f^{abd}}{8\pi^{5}}\, \frac{1}{k^{+}}\, \int_{x,y,z,x'}\,\log{\left(\frac{\vee}{k^{+}}\right)}\frac{(x'-w')\cdot (x-z)\, (y-z)\cdot (z-w)}{(x'-w')^{2}\, (y-z)^{2}\, (z-w)^{2}\,\left[(x-w)^{2} -(x-z)^{2}\right]}\, \notag \\ & \left[U^{ab'}(x')\,\rho^{b'}(x')-U^{ab'}(w')\, \rho^{b'}(x')\right]\times\,\left[U^{de}(x)\,U^{bc}(z)\, \rho^{e}(x)\,\rho^{c}(y)-U^{de}(x)\,U^{bc}(y)\,\rho^{e}(x)\,\rho^{c}(y)\right] \notag \\
& -\frac{ig^{4}\, f^{abd}}{8\pi^{5}}\, \frac{1}{k^{+}}\, \int_{x,y,z,x'}\,\log{\left(\frac{\vee}{k^{+}}\right)}\,\frac{(x'-w')\cdot (y-z)\, (z-w)\cdot (x-z)\,(x-w)^{2}}{(x'-w')^{2}\, (y-z)^{2}\, (z-w)^{2}\,\left[(x-w)^{2}-(x-z)^{2}\right]^{2}}\, \notag \\ & \left[U^{ab'}(x')\,\rho^{b'}(x')-U^{ab'}(w')\, \rho^{b'}(x')\right]\times\,\left[U^{de}(x)\,U^{bc}(z)\, \rho^{e}(x)\,\rho^{c}(y)-U^{de}(x)\,U^{bc}(y)\,\rho^{e}(x)\,\rho^{c}(y)\right]\Bigg\} \label{eq:B:G1IIIN2}
\end{align}

\paragraph{Row III, $d^3N_{3a}$.}
\old{Red}: the first logarithmic line, with the edge kernel $\frac{(x'-w')\cdot(y-z)}{(x'-w')^2(y-z)^2(z-w)^2}$, is not integrable at $z\to w$. \new{Blue}: it is replaced by $\mathcal D$ (fix F3, \texttt{BFKL\_final.pdf} Sec.~2.1): $U^{bc}(z)\to U^{bc}(w)$ and the kernel $\mathcal K(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal K(y-w)\,\mathcal K(x-z)\cdot\mathcal K(w-z)$. Without this change the three-$\rho$ terms do not cancel (Sec.~\ref{sec:three}).
\noindent Terms $\propto\log\frac{k^+}{\Lambda}$:
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N_{3a\,\rm NLO}}{d^{3}k} & = \frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\int_{w,w'}e^{-ik(w'-w)}\Bigg\{\notag \\
& \rc  -\frac{ig^{4}f^{abd}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\log{\left(\frac{k^{+}}{\Lambda}\right)}\,\int_{x,y,z,x'}
\frac{(x'-w')\cdot(y-z)}{(x'-w')^{2}(y-z)^{2}(z-w)^{2}}\notag \\ & \rc  \left[U^{ab'}(x')\,\rho^{b'}(x')-U^{ab'}(w')\, \rho^{b'}(x')\right]\times \left[U^{de}(x)\,U^{bc}(z)\, \rho^{e}(x)\,\rho^{c}(y)-U^{de}(x)\,U^{bc}(y)\,\rho^{e}(x)\,\rho^{c}(y)\right] \notag \\
& \nc  -\frac{ig^{4}f^{abd}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\log{\left(\frac{k^{+}}{\Lambda}\right)}\,\int_{x,y,z,x'}\frac{(x'-w')\cdot(y-w)\,(x-z)\cdot(w-z)}{(x'-w')^{2}\,(y-w)^{2}\,(x-z)^{2}\,(w-z)^{2}}\notag \\ & \nc  \left[U^{ab'}(x')\,\rho^{b'}(x')-U^{ab'}(w')\, \rho^{b'}(x')\right]\times \left[U^{de}(x)\,U^{bc}(w)\, \rho^{e}(x)\,\rho^{c}(y)-U^{de}(x)\,U^{bc}(y)\,\rho^{e}(x)\,\rho^{c}(y)\right] \notag \\
& -\frac{ig^{4}f^{abd}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\log{\left(\frac{k^{+}}{\Lambda}\right)}\,\,\int_{x,y,z,x'}
\frac{(x'-w')\cdot(y-z)\,(z-w)\cdot(x-w)\,(x-z)^{2}}{(x'-w')^{2}(y-z)^{2}(z-w)^{2}\left[(x-w)^{2}-(x-z)^{2}\right]^{2}}\notag \\ & \left[U^{ab'}(x')\,\rho^{b'}(x')-U^{ab'}(w')\, \rho^{b'}(x')\right]\times \left[U^{de}(x)\,U^{bc}(z)\, \rho^{e}(x)\,\rho^{c}(y)-U^{de}(x)\,U^{bc}(y)\,\rho^{e}(x)\,\rho^{c}(y)\right] \notag \\
& +\frac{ig^{4}f^{abd}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\log{\left(\frac{k^{+}}{\Lambda}\right)}\,\int_{x,y,z,x'}
\frac{(x'-w')\cdot(y-z)\,(z-w)\cdot(x-z)\,(x-w)^{2}}{(x'-w')^{2}(y-z)^{2}(z-w)^{2}\,\left[(x-w)^{2}-(x-z)^{2}\right]^{2}}\notag \\ & \left[U^{ab'}(x')\,\rho^{b'}(x')-U^{ab'}(w')\, \rho^{b'}(x')\right]\times \left[U^{de}(x)\,U^{bc}(z)\, \rho^{e}(x)\,\rho^{c}(y)-U^{de}(x)\,U^{bc}(y)\,\rho^{e}(x)\,\rho^{c}(y)\right]\Bigg\} \label{eq:J:G1IIIN3a}
\end{align}

\noindent Terms $\propto\log\frac{\vee}{k^+}$: none.

\paragraph{Row III, $d^3N_{3b}$.}
No large logarithm. ($\mathcal A$ and the logarithms of $d^3N_{3a}$, $d^3N_{3b}$ have finite limits for $\Lambda\to0$; \texttt{Remaining\_RC\_DGLAP\_finite.pdf}.)
\noindent Terms $\propto\log\frac{k^+}{\Lambda}$: none.

\noindent Terms $\propto\log\frac{\vee}{k^+}$: none.

\subsection{Row IV}
\paragraph{Row IV.}
\noindent Terms $\propto\log\frac{k^+}{\Lambda}$:
\begin{align}
    \frac{d^{3}N_{\rm NLO}^{\rm}}{d^{3}k}  & =   \frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,
    \int_{x,y,z,w,x',w'}\,e^{-ik\cdot (w^\prime-w)}\,\Bigg\{\notag \\
& + \frac{ig^{4}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\frac{(x'-w')^{i}\,(y-z)^{j}}{(x'-w')^{2}\,(y-z)^{2}}\times\,\log{\left(\frac{k^{+}}{\Lambda}\right)}\,\left[\frac{(x-w)^{i}\,(z-w)^{j}}{(z-w)^{2}\,(x-w)^{2}}+\frac{(x-z)^{j}\,(x-w)^{i}}{2\, (x-z)^{2}\,(x-w)^{2}}\right]\Bigg\}\times\notag \\ &  \left[U^{ab'}(x')\,\rho^{b'}(x')-U^{ab'}(w')\,\rho^{b'}(x')\right] \left[f^{cdc'}\,U^{ac}(w)\,U^{bd}(z)\,U^{be}(y)\,\rho^{e}(y)\rho^{c'}(x)-f^{abc}\,U^{be}(y)\,U^{cd}(x)\,\rho^{e}(y)\rho^{d}(x)\right] \label{eq:J:G1IV}
\end{align}

\noindent Terms $\propto\log\frac{\vee}{k^+}$:
\begin{align}
    \frac{d^{3}N_{\rm NLO}^{\rm}}{d^{3}k}  & =   \frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,
    \int_{x,y,z,w,x',w'}\,e^{-ik\cdot (w^\prime-w)}\,\Bigg\{\notag \\
& \frac{ig^{4}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\frac{(x'-w')^{i}\,(y-z)^{j}}{(x'-w')^{2}\,(y-z)^{2}}\times\,\log{\left(\frac{\vee}{k^{+}}\right)}\times\, \bigg[\frac{(x-z)^{j}\,(z-w)^{i}}{(z-w)^{2}\,(x-z)^{2}}\notag \\ & -\frac{(x-w)^{i}\,(z-w)^{j}}{(z-w)^{2}\,(x-w)^{2}}-\frac{(x-z)^{j}\,(x-w)^{i}}{(x-z)^{2}\,(x-w)^{2}}+\frac{\delta^{ij}}{2\,(z-w)^{2}}\bigg] \notag \\
& -\frac{ig^{4}}{16\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\frac{(x'-w')\cdot (y-z)}{(x'-w')^{2}\,(y-z)^{2}\,(z-w)^{2}}\times\,\log{\left(\frac{\vee}{k^{+}}\right)} \notag \\
& + \frac{ig^{4}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\frac{(x'-w')^{i}\,(y-z)^{j}}{(x'-w')^{2}\,(y-z)^{2}}\times\,\log{\left(\frac{\vee}{k^{+}}\right)}\,\left[\frac{(x-w)^{i}\,(z-w)^{j}}{(z-w)^{2}\,(x-w)^{2}}+\frac{(x-z)^{j}\,(x-w)^{i}}{2\, (x-z)^{2}\,(x-w)^{2}}\right]\Bigg\}\times\notag \\ &  \left[U^{ab'}(x')\,\rho^{b'}(x')-U^{ab'}(w')\,\rho^{b'}(x')\right] \left[f^{cdc'}\,U^{ac}(w)\,U^{bd}(z)\,U^{be}(y)\,\rho^{e}(y)\rho^{c'}(x)-f^{abc}\,U^{be}(y)\,U^{cd}(x)\,\rho^{e}(y)\rho^{d}(x)\right] \label{eq:B:G1IV}
\end{align}

\subsection{Row V}
\paragraph{Row V, first part.}
\noindent Terms $\propto\log\frac{k^+}{\Lambda}$:
\begin{align}
     \frac{d^{3}N_{1\,\rm NLO}^{\rm}}{d^{3}k}  & =   -\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\, \int_{x,z,y,x',y'w'}\,e^{-ik\cdot (w^\prime-z)}\,\frac{ig^{4}}{32\pi^{5}}\, \frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\log{\left(\frac{k^{+}}{\Lambda}\right)}\,\frac{(x'-w')\cdot (y-z)\,(z-x)\cdot (y'-x)}{(x'-w')^{2}\,(z-x)^{2}\,(y-z)^{2}\,(y'-x)^{2}}\,\notag \\ & \left[U^{ab'}(x')\,\rho^{b'}(x')-U^{ab'}(w')\,\rho^{b'}(x')\right]\times\, \bigg[f^{adc}\,U^{db}(x)\,U^{ce}(z)\, \{\rho^{e}(y),\rho^{b}(y')\}-f^{abc}\,U^{cd}(y)\,U^{be}(y')\, \{\rho^{d}(y),\rho^{e}(y')\}\bigg] \notag \\
&  -\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\, \int_{x,z,y,x',y'w'}\,e^{-ik\cdot (w^\prime-x)}\,\frac{ig^{4}}{32\pi^{5}}\, \frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\log{\left(\frac{k^{+}}{\Lambda}\right)}\,\frac{(x'-w')\cdot (y'-x)\,(z-x)\cdot (y-z)}{(x'-w')^{2}\,(z-x)^{2}\,(y-z)^{2}\,(y'-x)^{2}}\notag \\ & \times\, \left[U^{ab'}(x')\,\rho^{b'}(x')-U^{ab'}(w')\,\rho^{b'}(x')\right]\times \bigg[f^{adc}\,U^{db}(x)\,U^{ce}(z)\, \{\rho^{e}(y),\rho^{b}(y')\}-f^{abc}\,U^{cd}(y)\,U^{be}(y')\, \{\rho^{d}(y),\rho^{e}(y')\}\bigg] \label{eq:J:G1V1}
\end{align}

\noindent Terms $\propto\log\frac{\vee}{k^+}$: none.

\paragraph{Row V, second part, $d^3N_{2a}$.}
\noindent Terms $\propto\log\frac{k^+}{\Lambda}$:
\begin{align}
    \frac{d^{3}N_{2a\,\rm NLO}^{\rm}}{d^{3}k}  & =    - \frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\, \frac{g^{4}}{16\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\log{\left(\frac{k^{+}}{\Lambda}\right)}\, \int_{x,y,z,x',w'}\, e^{-ik\cdot(w'-z)}\,\frac{(x'-w')^{i}\,(z-x)^{m}}{(x'-w')^{2}\,(z-x)^{2}\, (y-z)^{2}}\,\Bigg[\frac{(y-z)^{i}\,(x-z)^{m}}{(x-z)^{2}}\notag \\ & +\frac{(y-x)^{m}\,(y-z)^{i}}{2\,(y-x)^{2}}\Bigg]\times\, \Bigg[U^{ab'}(x')-U^{ab'}(w')\Bigg]\,\Bigg[-f^{adc}\,f^{fbe}\,U^{db}(x)\,U^{ce}(z)+N_{c}\,U^{af}(x)\Bigg]\,\rho^{b'}(x')\,\rho^{f}(y) \notag \\
& - \frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\, \frac{g^{4}}{16\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\log{\left(\frac{k^{+}}{\Lambda}\right)}\, \int_{x,y,z,x',w'}\,e^{-ik\cdot(w'-x)}\,\frac{(x'-w')^{i}\,(z-x)^{m}}{(x'-w')^{2}\,(z-x)^{2}\,(y-x)^{2}} \notag \\ &\left[\frac{(y-x)^{i}\,(x-z)^{m}}{(x-z)^{2}}-\frac{(y-x)^{i}\,(y-z)^{m}}{2\,(y-z)^{2}}\right]\, \Bigg[U^{ab'}(x')-U^{ab'}(w')\Bigg]\,\Bigg[-f^{adc}\,f^{fbe}\,U^{db}(x)\,U^{ce}(z)+N_{c}\,U^{af}(x)\Bigg]\,\rho^{b'}(x')\,\rho^{f}(y) \label{eq:J:G1V2a}
\end{align}

\noindent Terms $\propto\log\frac{\vee}{k^+}$: none.

\paragraph{Row V, second part, $d^3N_{2b\,\rm UV}$.}
Your bracket $\big[2\log\frac{k^+}{\Lambda}+2\log(1-\frac{\Lambda}{k^+})-\frac{11}{6}\big]$ is correct (E1 is now in your file; Row V has $0<p^+<k^+$, so no $\vee$). The poles of the $\log\frac{k^+}{\Lambda}$ terms cancel: Group I Row II : Row V 2b UV : Group II Row IV $=1:-2:1$, sum $8.2\times10^{-16}$ (relative; \texttt{ll\_checks.py poles}).
\noindent Terms $\propto\log\frac{k^+}{\Lambda}$:
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N_{2b\,\rm UV\, NLO}^{\rm}}{d^{3}k} & =  \frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}N_{c}}{16\pi^{4}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}
\new{2}\,\log{\left(\frac{k^{+}}{\Lambda}\right)}\int_{y,z,x',w'}e^{-ik\cdot(w'-z)}\notag \\ & \left[\frac{2}{\epsilon}+2\gamma_{E}+\log\frac{(y-z)^{2}\mu^{2}_{\overline{MS}}}{4}\right]
\frac{(x'-w')\cdot(y-z)}{(x'-w')^{2}\,(y-z)^{2}}\, \Big[U^{ab'}(x')-U^{ab'}(w')\Big]\Big[U^{af}(y)-U^{af}(z)\Big]\rho^{b'}(x')\,\rho^{f}(y). \label{eq:J:G1V2bUV}
\end{align}

\noindent Terms $\propto\log\frac{\vee}{k^+}$: none.

\paragraph{Row V, second part, $d^3N_{2b\,\rm finite}$.}
Lines 4--6 carry $\log\frac{k^+-\Lambda}{\Lambda}=\log\frac{k^+}{\Lambda}+\log(1-\frac{\Lambda}{k^+})$.
\noindent Terms $\propto\log\frac{k^+}{\Lambda}$:
\begin{align}
    \frac{d^{3}N_{2b\,\rm finite\, NLO}^{\rm}}{d^{3}k} & =   -\, \frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{16\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\log{\left(\frac{k^{+}}{\Lambda}\right)}\,\int_{x,y,z,x',w'}e^{-ik\cdot(w'-x)}\, \bigg[\frac{(y-x)\cdot (x'-w')\,(x-z)\cdot (z-x)}{(x-z)^{2}\,(x'-w')^{2}\,(z-x)^{2}\,(y-x)^{2}}\notag \\ & -\frac{(y-x)\cdot (x'-w')\,(y-z)\cdot (z-x)}{2(y-z)^{2}\,(x'-w')^{2}\,(z-x)^{2}\,(y-x)^{2}}\bigg]\,\times \Big[U^{ab'}(x')-U^{ab'}(w')\Big]\Big[N_{c}\,U^{af}(y)-N_{c}\,U^{af}(x)\Big]\rho^{b'}(x')\,\rho^{f}(y) \notag \\
& -\, \frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{16\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\log{\left(\frac{k^{+}}{\Lambda}\right)}\,\int_{x,y,z,x',w'}e^{-ik\cdot(w'-z)}\,\bigg[\frac{(y-z)\cdot (x'-w')\,(x-z)\cdot (z-x)}{(x-z)^{2}\,(x'-w')^{2}\,(z-x)^{2}\,(y-z)^{2}}\notag \\ & +\frac{(y-x)\cdot (z-x)\,(y-z)\cdot (x'-w')}{2(y-x)^{2}\,(x'-w')^{2}\,(z-x)^{2}\,(y-z)^{2}}\bigg]\,\times \Big[U^{ab'}(x')-U^{ab'}(w')\Big]\Big[N_{c}\,U^{af}(y)-N_{c}\,U^{af}(x)\Big]\rho^{b'}(x')\,\rho^{f}(y) \notag \\
& - \frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{16\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\int_{x,y,z,x',w'}e^{-ik\cdot(w'-z)}\,2\,\log{\left(\frac{k^{+}}{\Lambda}\right)}\notag \\ & \frac{(x'-w')\cdot(y-z)}{(x'-w')^{2}\,(y-z)^{2}}\,\frac{(y-x)\cdot(y-z)}{(y-x)^{2}\,(x-z)^{2}}\times\Big[U^{ab'}(x')-U^{ab'}(w')\Big]\Big[N_{c}\,U^{af}(y)-N_{c}\,U^{af}(z)\Big]\rho^{b'}(x')\,\rho^{f}(y) \label{eq:J:G1V2bfin}
\end{align}

\noindent Terms $\propto\log\frac{\vee}{k^+}$: none.

\section{Group II (no c.c.)}\label{sec:LLgII}
\subsection{Row I}
\paragraph{Row I, first part, first line.}
\noindent Terms $\propto\log\frac{k^+}{\Lambda}$:
\begin{align}
 \frac{d^{3}N_{1\,\rm NLO}^{\rm}}{d^{3}k}  & =     \frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\int_{x,y,z,w,x',w^{\prime}} e^{-ik(w^{\prime}-w)}\Bigg\{\notag \\
& -\, \frac{ig^{4}}{16\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\log{\left(\frac{k^{+}}{\Lambda}\right)}\,\frac{(x'-w')^{i}\,(y-z)^{j}}{(x'-w')^{2}\,(y-z)^{2}}\,\left[\frac{(x-w)^{i}\,(z-w)^{j}}{(z-w)^{2}\,(x-w)^{2}}+\frac{(x-z)^{j}\,(x-w)^{i}}{2\,(x-z)^{2}\, (x-w)^{2}}\right]\Bigg\} \notag \\ & \Bigg[U^{ac'}(w')\,\{\rho^{c'}(x'),\rho^{b}(y)\}-U^{bc'}(z)\,U^{ad'}(x')\,U^{c'e'}(y)\, \{\rho^{d'}(x'),\rho^{e'}(y)\}\Bigg]\times \Bigg[f^{cbd}\,U^{ac}(w)\,\rho^{d}(x)-f^{acd}\, U^{bc}(z)\,U^{de}(x)\,\rho^{e}(x)\Bigg]\notag \\ \label{eq:J:G2I1A}
\end{align}

\noindent Terms $\propto\log\frac{\vee}{k^+}$:
\begin{align}
 \frac{d^{3}N_{1\,\rm NLO}^{\rm}}{d^{3}k}  & =     \frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\int_{x,y,z,w,x',w^{\prime}} e^{-ik(w^{\prime}-w)}\Bigg\{\notag \\
& -\, \frac{ig^{4}}{16\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\, \frac{(x'-w')^{i}\,(y-z)^{j}}{(x'-w')^{2}\,(y-z)^{2}}\,\log{\left(\frac{\vee}{k^{+}}\right)}\times\notag \\ &  \Bigg[\frac{\delta^{ij}}{2\,(z-w)^{2}}+\frac{(x-z)^{j}\,(z-w)^{i}}{(z-w)^{2}\,(x-z)^{2}}-\frac{(x-z)^{j}\,(x-w)^{i}}{(x-w)^{2}\,(x-z)^{2}}-\frac{(x-w)^{i}\,(z-w)^{j}}{(z-w)^{2}\,(x-w)^{2}}\Bigg] \notag \\
& +\,\frac{ig^{4}}{32\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\log{\left(\frac{\vee}{k^{+}}\right)}\, \frac{(x'-w')\cdot (y-z)}{(x'-w')^{2}\,(y-z)^{2}\,(z-w)^{2}} \notag \\
& -\, \frac{ig^{4}}{16\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\log{\left(\frac{\vee}{k^{+}}\right)}\,\frac{(x'-w')^{i}\,(y-z)^{j}}{(x'-w')^{2}\,(y-z)^{2}}\,\left[\frac{(x-w)^{i}\,(z-w)^{j}}{(z-w)^{2}\,(x-w)^{2}}+\frac{(x-z)^{j}\,(x-w)^{i}}{2\,(x-z)^{2}\, (x-w)^{2}}\right]\Bigg\} \notag \\ & \Bigg[U^{ac'}(w')\,\{\rho^{c'}(x'),\rho^{b}(y)\}-U^{bc'}(z)\,U^{ad'}(x')\,U^{c'e'}(y)\, \{\rho^{d'}(x'),\rho^{e'}(y)\}\Bigg]\times \Bigg[f^{cbd}\,U^{ac}(w)\,\rho^{d}(x)-f^{acd}\, U^{bc}(z)\,U^{de}(x)\,\rho^{e}(x)\Bigg]\notag \\ \label{eq:B:G2I1A}
\end{align}

\paragraph{Row I, first part, second line.}
\noindent Terms $\propto\log\frac{k^+}{\Lambda}$:
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N}{d^{3}k} & =  \frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\int_{x,y,z,w,x',w^{\prime}} e^{-ik(w^{\prime}-w)}\Bigg\{\notag \\
& +\,\frac{ig^{4}}{16\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\log{\left(\frac{k^{+}}{\Lambda}\right)}\,\frac{(x'-w)^{i}\,(y-z)^{j}}{(x'-w)^{2}\,(y-z)^{2}}\,\left[\frac{(x-w')^{i}\,(z-w')^{j}}{(z-w')^{2}\,(x-w')^{2}}+\frac{(x-z)^{j}\,(x-w')^{i}}{2\,(x-z)^{2}\, (x-w')^{2}}\right]\Bigg\} \notag \\ & \times\Bigg[f^{c'bd'}\,U^{ac'}(w')\,\rho^{d'}(x)-f^{ac'd'}\, U^{bc'}(z)\,U^{d'e'}(x)\,\rho^{e'}(x)\Bigg] \times \Bigg[U^{ac}(w)\,\{\rho^{c}(x'),\rho^{b}(y)\}-U^{bc}(z)\,U^{ad}(x')\,U^{ce}(y)\, \{\rho^{d}(x'),\rho^{e}(y)\}\Bigg] \label{eq:J:G2I1B}
\end{align}

\noindent Terms $\propto\log\frac{\vee}{k^+}$:
\begin{align}
\frac{d^{3}N}{d^{3}k} & =  \frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\int_{x,y,z,w,x',w^{\prime}} e^{-ik(w^{\prime}-w)}\Bigg\{\notag \\
&  \frac{ig^{4}}{16\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\, \frac{(x'-w)^{i}\,(y-z)^{j}}{(x'-w)^{2}\,(y-z)^{2}}\,\log{\left(\frac{\vee}{k^{+}}\right)}\times\notag \\ &  \Bigg[\frac{\delta^{ij}}{2\,(z-w')^{2}}+\frac{(x-z)^{j}\,(z-w')^{i}}{(z-w')^{2}\,(x-z)^{2}}-\frac{(x-z)^{j}\,(x-w')^{i}}{(x-w')^{2}\,(x-z)^{2}}-\frac{(x-w')^{i}\,(z-w')^{j}}{(z-w')^{2}\,(x-w')^{2}}\Bigg] \notag \\
& -\,\frac{ig^{4}}{32\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\log{\left(\frac{\vee}{k^{+}}\right)}\, \frac{(x'-w)\cdot (y-z)}{(x'-w)^{2}\,(y-z)^{2}\,(z-w')^{2}} \notag \\
& +\,\frac{ig^{4}}{16\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\log{\left(\frac{\vee}{k^{+}}\right)}\,\frac{(x'-w)^{i}\,(y-z)^{j}}{(x'-w)^{2}\,(y-z)^{2}}\,\left[\frac{(x-w')^{i}\,(z-w')^{j}}{(z-w')^{2}\,(x-w')^{2}}+\frac{(x-z)^{j}\,(x-w')^{i}}{2\,(x-z)^{2}\, (x-w')^{2}}\right]\Bigg\} \notag \\ & \times\Bigg[f^{c'bd'}\,U^{ac'}(w')\,\rho^{d'}(x)-f^{ac'd'}\, U^{bc'}(z)\,U^{d'e'}(x)\,\rho^{e'}(x)\Bigg] \times \Bigg[U^{ac}(w)\,\{\rho^{c}(x'),\rho^{b}(y)\}-U^{bc}(z)\,U^{ad}(x')\,U^{ce}(y)\, \{\rho^{d}(x'),\rho^{e}(y)\}\Bigg] \label{eq:B:G2I1B}
\end{align}

\paragraph{Row I, second part, $d^3N_{2a}$.}
No large logarithm: $\vee$ cancels between numerator and denominator of the first logarithm, and the second has the finite limit $\log\frac{(x-w)^2}{(x'-w')^2}$.
\noindent Terms $\propto\log\frac{k^+}{\Lambda}$: none.

\noindent Terms $\propto\log\frac{\vee}{k^+}$: none.

\paragraph{Row I, second part, $d^3N_{2b}$.}
\noindent Terms $\propto\log\frac{k^+}{\Lambda}$:
\begin{align}
    \frac{d^{3}N_{2b\,\rm NLO}^{\rm}}{d^{3}k}  & =  \frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\, \frac{g^{4}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\log{\left(\frac{k^{+}}{\Lambda}\right)}\,\int_{z,w,w^{\prime},x,x'}\, e^{-ik(w^{\prime}-w)}\, \frac{1}{\left[(x'-w')^{2}\,(x-z)^{2}\,(x-w)^{2} - (x-w)^{4}\, (x'-z)^{2}\right]} \times\notag \\ & \Bigg[\frac{(x'-w')\cdot (x-w)\, (z-w)\cdot (z-w')}{(z-w)^{2}\, (z-w')^{2}}+\frac{(x'-w')\cdot (x-w)\, (x'-z)\cdot (z-w)}{2\, (z-w)^{2}\, (x'-z)^{2}}\notag \\ & +\frac{(x-w)\cdot (x'-w')\, (x-z)\cdot (z-w')}{2\, (x-z)^{2}\, (z-w')^{2}}+\frac{(x-z)\cdot (x'-z)\, (x'-w')\cdot (x-w)}{4\, (x-z)^{2}\, (x'-z)^{2}}\Bigg] \times\,\Bigg[(x-z)^{2} - \frac{(x-w)^{2}\, (x'-z)^{2}}{(x'-w')^{2}}\Bigg]\,\times \notag \\ & \Bigg[f^{c'bd'}\,f^{cbd}\, U^{ac'}(w')\,U^{ac}(w)\,\rho^{d'}(x')\rho^{d}(x)- f^{c'bd'}\,f^{acd}\,U^{ac'}(w')\,U^{bc}(z)\,U^{de}(x)\,\rho^{d'}(x')\rho^{e}(x) \notag \\ & -f^{ac'd'}\,f^{cbd}\,U^{ac}(w)\,U^{bc'}(z)\,U^{e'd'}(x')\,\rho^{e'}(x')\,\rho^{d}(x) +N_{c}\,U^{e'd}(x')\,U^{de}(x)\,\rho^{e'}(x')\,\rho^{e}(x)\Bigg] \label{eq:J:G2I2b}
\end{align}

\noindent Terms $\propto\log\frac{\vee}{k^+}$:
\begin{align}
    \frac{d^{3}N_{2b\,\rm NLO}^{\rm}}{d^{3}k}  & =  \frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\, \frac{g^{4}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\log{\left(\frac{\vee}{k^{+}}\right)}\,\int_{z,w,w^{\prime},x,x'}\, e^{-ik(w^{\prime}-w)}\, \frac{1}{\left[(x'-w')^{2}\,(x-z)^{2}\,(x-w)^{2} - (x-w)^{4}\, (x'-z)^{2}\right]} \times\notag \\ & \Bigg[\frac{(x'-w')\cdot (x-w)\, (z-w)\cdot (z-w')}{(z-w)^{2}\, (z-w')^{2}}+\frac{(x'-w')\cdot (x-w)\, (x'-z)\cdot (z-w)}{2\, (z-w)^{2}\, (x'-z)^{2}}\notag \\ & +\frac{(x-w)\cdot (x'-w')\, (x-z)\cdot (z-w')}{2\, (x-z)^{2}\, (z-w')^{2}}+\frac{(x-z)\cdot (x'-z)\, (x'-w')\cdot (x-w)}{4\, (x-z)^{2}\, (x'-z)^{2}}\Bigg] \times\,\Bigg[(x-z)^{2} - \frac{(x-w)^{2}\, (x'-z)^{2}}{(x'-w')^{2}}\Bigg]\,\times \notag \\ & \Bigg[f^{c'bd'}\,f^{cbd}\, U^{ac'}(w')\,U^{ac}(w)\,\rho^{d'}(x')\rho^{d}(x)- f^{c'bd'}\,f^{acd}\,U^{ac'}(w')\,U^{bc}(z)\,U^{de}(x)\,\rho^{d'}(x')\rho^{e}(x) \notag \\ & -f^{ac'd'}\,f^{cbd}\,U^{ac}(w)\,U^{bc'}(z)\,U^{e'd'}(x')\,\rho^{e'}(x')\,\rho^{d}(x) +N_{c}\,U^{e'd}(x')\,U^{de}(x)\,\rho^{e'}(x')\,\rho^{e}(x)\Bigg] \notag \\
&  - \frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\, \frac{g^{4}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\int_{z,w,w^{\prime},x,x'}\, e^{-ik(w^{\prime}-w)}\, \frac{(x-z)^{2}}{\left[(x'-w')^{2}\,(x-z)^{2}\,(x-w)^{2} - (x-w)^{4}\, (x'-z)^{2}\right]}\,  \times\notag \\ & \Bigg[\frac{(x'-w')\cdot (x-w)\, (z-w)\cdot (z-w')}{(z-w)^{2}\, (z-w')^{2}}+\frac{(x'-w')\cdot (x-w)\, (x'-z)\cdot (z-w)}{2\, (z-w)^{2}\, (x'-z)^{2}}\notag \\ & +\frac{(x-w)\cdot (x'-w')\, (x-z)\cdot (z-w')}{2\, (x-z)^{2}\, (z-w')^{2}}+\frac{(x-z)\cdot (x'-z)\, (x'-w')\cdot (x-w)}{4\, (x-z)^{2}\, (x'-z)^{2}}\Bigg] \times\,\log{\left(\frac{\vee}{k^{+}}\right)}\,\times \notag \\ & \Bigg[f^{c'bd'}\,f^{cbd}\, U^{ac'}(w')\,U^{ac}(w)\,\rho^{d'}(x')\rho^{d}(x)- f^{c'bd'}\,f^{acd}\,U^{ac'}(w')\,U^{bc}(z)\,U^{de}(x)\,\rho^{d'}(x')\rho^{e}(x) \notag \\ & -f^{ac'd'}\,f^{cbd}\,U^{ac}(w)\,U^{bc'}(z)\,U^{e'd'}(x')\,\rho^{e'}(x')\,\rho^{d}(x) +N_{c}\,U^{e'd}(x')\,U^{de}(x)\,\rho^{e'}(x')\,\rho^{e}(x)\Bigg] \notag \\
&  + \frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\, \frac{g^{4}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\int_{z,w,w^{\prime},x,x'}\, e^{-ik(w^{\prime}-w)}\, \frac{(x'-z)^{2}}{\left[(x'-w')^{4}\,(x-z)^{2}\, - (x-w)^{2}\, (x'-z)^{2}\,(x'-w')^{2}\right]}\,  \times\notag \\ & \Bigg[\frac{(x'-w')\cdot (x-w)\, (z-w)\cdot (z-w')}{(z-w)^{2}\, (z-w')^{2}}+\frac{(x'-w')\cdot (x-w)\, (x'-z)\cdot (z-w)}{2\, (z-w)^{2}\, (x'-z)^{2}}\notag \\ & +\frac{(x-w)\cdot (x'-w')\, (x-z)\cdot (z-w')}{2\, (x-z)^{2}\, (z-w')^{2}}+\frac{(x-z)\cdot (x'-z)\, (x'-w')\cdot (x-w)}{4\, (x-z)^{2}\, (x'-z)^{2}}\Bigg] \times\,\log{\left(\frac{\vee}{k^{+}}\right)} \notag \\ & \Bigg[f^{c'bd'}\,f^{cbd}\, U^{ac'}(w')\,U^{ac}(w)\,\rho^{d'}(x')\rho^{d}(x)- f^{c'bd'}\,f^{acd}\,U^{ac'}(w')\,U^{bc}(z)\,U^{de}(x)\,\rho^{d'}(x')\rho^{e}(x) \notag \\ & -f^{ac'd'}\,f^{cbd}\,U^{ac}(w)\,U^{bc'}(z)\,U^{e'd'}(x')\,\rho^{e'}(x')\,\rho^{d}(x) +N_{c}\,U^{e'd}(x')\,U^{de}(x)\,\rho^{e'}(x')\,\rho^{e}(x)\Bigg] \label{eq:B:G2I2b}
\end{align}

\paragraph{Row I, second part, $d^3N_{2c}$.}
\noindent Terms $\propto\log\frac{k^+}{\Lambda}$: none.

\noindent Terms $\propto\log\frac{\vee}{k^+}$:
\begin{align}
    \frac{d^{3}N_{2c\,\rm NLO}^{\rm}}{d^{3}k}  & =  \frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\, \frac{g^{4}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\int_{z,w,w^{\prime},x,x'}\, e^{-ik(w^{\prime}-w)}\,\frac{(x'-w')^{2}}{(x'-z)^{2}}\, \frac{1}{\left[(x'-w')^{2}\, (x-z)^{2} - (x-w)^{2}\, (x'-z)^{2}\right]} \notag \\ & \Bigg[\frac{(x'-z)\cdot (x-z)\, (z-w)\cdot (z-w')}{(z-w)^{2}\, (z-w')^{2}}-\frac{(x'-z)\cdot (x-z)\, (x'-w')\cdot (z-w)}{2\, (z-w)^{2}\, (x'-w')^{2}} -\frac{(x-w)\cdot (z-w')\, (x-z)\cdot (x'-z)}{2\, (x-w)^{2}\, (z-w')^{2}}\notag \\ & +\frac{(x-z)\cdot (x'-z)\, (x'-w')\cdot (x-w)}{4\, (x-w)^{2}\, (x'-w')^{2}}\Bigg]\,\times\, \log{\left(\frac{\vee}{k^{+}}\right)} \notag \\ &  \Bigg[f^{c'bd'}\,f^{cbd}\, U^{ac'}(w')\,U^{ac}(w)\,\rho^{d'}(x')\rho^{d}(x)- f^{c'bd'}\,f^{acd}\,U^{ac'}(w')\,U^{bc}(z)\,U^{de}(x)\,\rho^{d'}(x')\rho^{e}(x) \notag \\ & -f^{ac'd'}\,f^{cbd}\,U^{ac}(w)\,U^{bc'}(z)\,U^{e'd'}(x')\,\rho^{e'}(x')\,\rho^{d}(x) +N_{c}\,U^{e'd}(x')\,U^{de}(x)\,\rho^{e'}(x')\,\rho^{e}(x)\Bigg] \notag \\
& -\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\, \frac{g^{4}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\int_{z,w,w^{\prime},x,x'}\, e^{-ik(w^{\prime}-w)}\,\frac{(x-w)^{2}}{(x-z)^{2}}\, \frac{1}{\left[(x'-w')^{2}\, (x-z)^{2} - (x-w)^{2}\, (x'-z)^{2}\right]} \notag \\ & \Bigg[\frac{(x'-z)\cdot (x-z)\, (z-w)\cdot (z-w')}{(z-w)^{2}\, (z-w')^{2}}-\frac{(x'-z)\cdot (x-z)\, (x'-w')\cdot (z-w)}{2\, (z-w)^{2}\, (x'-w')^{2}} -\frac{(x-w)\cdot (z-w')\, (x-z)\cdot (x'-z)}{2\, (x-w)^{2}\, (z-w')^{2}}\notag \\ & +\frac{(x-z)\cdot (x'-z)\, (x'-w')\cdot (x-w)}{4\, (x-w)^{2}\, (x'-w')^{2}}\Bigg]\,\times\, \log{\left(\frac{\vee}{k^{+}}\right)} \notag \\ &  \Bigg[f^{c'bd'}\,f^{cbd}\, U^{ac'}(w')\,U^{ac}(w)\,\rho^{d'}(x')\rho^{d}(x)- f^{c'bd'}\,f^{acd}\,U^{ac'}(w')\,U^{bc}(z)\,U^{de}(x)\,\rho^{d'}(x')\rho^{e}(x) \notag \\ & -f^{ac'd'}\,f^{cbd}\,U^{ac}(w)\,U^{bc'}(z)\,U^{e'd'}(x')\,\rho^{e'}(x')\,\rho^{d}(x) +N_{c}\,U^{e'd}(x')\,U^{de}(x)\,\rho^{e'}(x')\,\rho^{e}(x)\Bigg] \label{eq:B:G2I2c}
\end{align}

\paragraph{Row I, second part, $d^3N_{2d}$.}
\noindent Terms $\propto\log\frac{k^+}{\Lambda}$: none.

\noindent Terms $\propto\log\frac{\vee}{k^+}$:
\begin{align}
 \frac{d^{3}N_{2d\,\rm NLO}^{\rm}}{d^{3}k}  & =  -\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\, \frac{g^{4}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\log{\left(\frac{\vee}{k^{+}}\right)}\,\int_{z,w,w^{\prime},x,x'}\, e^{-ik(w^{\prime}-w)}\,\frac{1}{\left[(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\right]}\, \frac{1}{\left[(x'-w')^{2}-(x'-z)^{2}\right]}\notag \\ & \Bigg[\frac{(x-w)\cdot (z-w)\, \left[(x'-z)^{2}-(x'-w')^{2}\right]}{2\, (z-w)^{2}\, (z-w')^{2}}  + \frac{(x-z)\cdot (x-w)\,\left[(x'-z)^{2}-(x'-w')^{2}\right] }{4\,(x-z)^{2}\,(z-w')^{2}}\notag \\ & +\frac{(x'-w')\cdot (z-w')\, \left[(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\right]}{2\, (z-w)^{2}\, (z-w')^{2}}+ \frac{(x'-z)\cdot (x'-w')\, \left[(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\right]}{4\, (z-w)^{2}\,(x'-z)^{2}}\Bigg] \notag \\ &  \Bigg[f^{c'bd'}\,f^{cbd}\, U^{ac'}(w')\,U^{ac}(w)\,\rho^{d'}(x')\rho^{d}(x)- f^{c'bd'}\,f^{acd}\,U^{ac'}(w')\,U^{bc}(z)\,U^{de}(x)\,\rho^{d'}(x')\rho^{e}(x) \notag \\ & -f^{ac'd'}\,f^{cbd}\,U^{ac}(w)\,U^{bc'}(z)\,U^{e'd'}(x')\,\rho^{e'}(x')\,\rho^{d}(x) +N_{c}\,U^{e'd}(x')\,U^{de}(x)\,\rho^{e'}(x')\,\rho^{e}(x)\Bigg]  \notag \\
& +\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\, \frac{g^{4}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\int_{z,w,w^{\prime},x,x'}\, e^{-ik(w^{\prime}-w)}\,\frac{(x-z)^{2}}{\left[(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\right]}\,\frac{1}{\left[(x'-w')^{2}\,(x-z)^{2} - (x-w)^{2}\,(x'-z)^{2}\right]}\notag \\ & \Bigg[\frac{(x-w)\cdot (z-w)\, \left[(x'-z)^{2}-(x'-w')^{2}\right]}{2\, (z-w)^{2}\, (z-w')^{2}}  + \frac{(x-z)\cdot (x-w)\,\left[(x'-z)^{2}-(x'-w')^{2}\right] }{4\,(x-z)^{2}\,(z-w')^{2}}\notag \\ & +\frac{(x'-w')\cdot (z-w')\, \left[(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\right]}{2\, (z-w)^{2}\, (z-w')^{2}}+ \frac{(x'-z)\cdot (x'-w')\, \left[(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\right]}{4\, (z-w)^{2}\,(x'-z)^{2}}\Bigg]\times\,\notag \\ & \log{\left(\frac{\vee}{k^{+}}\right)} \times\notag \\ & \Bigg[f^{c'bd'}\,f^{cbd}\, U^{ac'}(w')\,U^{ac}(w)\,\rho^{d'}(x')\rho^{d}(x)- f^{c'bd'}\,f^{acd}\,U^{ac'}(w')\,U^{bc}(z)\,U^{de}(x)\,\rho^{d'}(x')\rho^{e}(x) \notag \\ & -f^{ac'd'}\,f^{cbd}\,U^{ac}(w)\,U^{bc'}(z)\,U^{e'd'}(x')\,\rho^{e'}(x')\,\rho^{d}(x) +N_{c}\,U^{e'd}(x')\,U^{de}(x)\,\rho^{e'}(x')\,\rho^{e}(x)\Bigg] \notag \\
& +\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\, \frac{g^{4}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\int_{z,w,w^{\prime},x,x'}\, e^{-ik(w^{\prime}-w)}\,\frac{(x'-z)^{2}}{\left[(x'-w')^{2}-(x'-z)^{2}\right]}\,\frac{1}{\left[(x'-w')^{2}\,(x-z)^{2} - (x-w)^{2}\,(x'-z)^{2}\right]}\notag \\ & \Bigg[\frac{(x-w)\cdot (z-w)\, \left[(x'-z)^{2}-(x'-w')^{2}\right]}{2\, (z-w)^{2}\, (z-w')^{2}}  + \frac{(x-z)\cdot (x-w)\,\left[(x'-z)^{2}-(x'-w')^{2}\right] }{4\,(x-z)^{2}\,(z-w')^{2}}\notag \\ & +\frac{(x'-w')\cdot (z-w')\, \left[(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\right]}{2\, (z-w)^{2}\, (z-w')^{2}}+ \frac{(x'-z)\cdot (x'-w')\, \left[(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\right]}{4\, (z-w)^{2}\,(x'-z)^{2}}\Bigg]\times\,\notag \\ & \log{\left(\frac{\vee}{k^{+}}\right)} \times\notag \\ & \Bigg[f^{c'bd'}\,f^{cbd}\, U^{ac'}(w')\,U^{ac}(w)\,\rho^{d'}(x')\rho^{d}(x)- f^{c'bd'}\,f^{acd}\,U^{ac'}(w')\,U^{bc}(z)\,U^{de}(x)\,\rho^{d'}(x')\rho^{e}(x) \notag \\ & -f^{ac'd'}\,f^{cbd}\,U^{ac}(w)\,U^{bc'}(z)\,U^{e'd'}(x')\,\rho^{e'}(x')\,\rho^{d}(x) +N_{c}\,U^{e'd}(x')\,U^{de}(x)\,\rho^{e'}(x')\,\rho^{e}(x)\Bigg] \label{eq:B:G2I2d}
\end{align}

\paragraph{Row I, second part, $d^3N_{2e}$.}
\noindent Terms $\propto\log\frac{k^+}{\Lambda}$: none.

\noindent Terms $\propto\log\frac{\vee}{k^+}$:
\begin{align}
 \frac{d^{3}N_{2e\,\rm NLO}^{\rm}}{d^{3}k}  & =  \frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\, \frac{g^{4}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\log{\left(\frac{\vee}{k^{+}}\right)}\,\int_{z,w,w^{\prime},x,x'}\, e^{-ik(w^{\prime}-w)}\, \frac{1}{\left[(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\right]}\, \frac{1}{\left[(x'-w')^{2} - (x'-z)^{2}\right]} \notag \\ & \Bigg[\frac{(x-z)\cdot (z-w)\, \left[(x'-z)^{2}-(x'-w')^{2}\right]}{2\, (z-w)^{2}\, (z-w')^{2}}  - \frac{(x-z)\cdot (x-w)\,\left[(x'-z)^{2}-(x'-w')^{2}\right] }{4\,(x-w)^{2}\,(z-w')^{2}}\notag \\ & +\frac{(x'-z)\cdot (z-w')\, \left[(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\right]}{2\, (z-w)^{2}\, (z-w')^{2}}-\frac{(x'-z)\cdot (x'-w')\, \left[(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\right]}{4\, (z-w)^{2}\,(x'-w')^{2}}\Bigg]\notag \\ & \Bigg[f^{c'bd'}\,f^{cbd}\, U^{ac'}(w')\,U^{ac}(w)\,\rho^{d'}(x')\rho^{d}(x)- f^{c'bd'}\,f^{acd}\,U^{ac'}(w')\,U^{bc}(z)\,U^{de}(x)\,\rho^{d'}(x')\rho^{e}(x) \notag \\ & -f^{ac'd'}\,f^{cbd}\,U^{ac}(w)\,U^{bc'}(z)\,U^{e'd'}(x')\,\rho^{e'}(x')\,\rho^{d}(x) +N_{c}\,U^{e'd}(x')\,U^{de}(x)\,\rho^{e'}(x')\,\rho^{e}(x)\Bigg] \notag \\
& -\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\, \frac{g^{4}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\int_{z,w,w^{\prime},x,x'}\, e^{-ik(w^{\prime}-w)}\,\frac{(x-w)^{2}}{\left[(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\right]}\, \frac{1}{\left[(x'-w')^{2}\, (x-z)^{2} - (x-w)^{2}\, (x'-z)^{2}\right]} \notag \\ & \Bigg[\frac{(x-z)\cdot (z-w)\, \left[(x'-z)^{2}-(x'-w')^{2}\right]}{2\, (z-w)^{2}\, (z-w')^{2}}  - \frac{(x-z)\cdot (x-w)\,\left[(x'-z)^{2}-(x'-w')^{2}\right] }{4\,(x-w)^{2}\,(z-w')^{2}}\notag \\ & +\frac{(x'-z)\cdot (z-w')\, \left[(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\right]}{2\, (z-w)^{2}\, (z-w')^{2}}-\frac{(x'-z)\cdot (x'-w')\, \left[(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\right]}{4\, (z-w)^{2}\,(x'-w')^{2}}\Bigg]\times\,\notag \\ & \log{\left(\frac{\vee}{k^{+}}\right)}\times\,\notag \\ & \Bigg[f^{c'bd'}\,f^{cbd}\, U^{ac'}(w')\,U^{ac}(w)\,\rho^{d'}(x')\rho^{d}(x)- f^{c'bd'}\,f^{acd}\,U^{ac'}(w')\,U^{bc}(z)\,U^{de}(x)\,\rho^{d'}(x')\rho^{e}(x) \notag \\ & -f^{ac'd'}\,f^{cbd}\,U^{ac}(w)\,U^{bc'}(z)\,U^{e'd'}(x')\,\rho^{e'}(x')\,\rho^{d}(x) +N_{c}\,U^{e'd}(x')\,U^{de}(x)\,\rho^{e'}(x')\,\rho^{e}(x)\Bigg] \notag \\
& -\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\, \frac{g^{4}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\int_{z,w,w^{\prime},x,x'}\, e^{-ik(w^{\prime}-w)}\, \frac{(x'-w')^{2}}{\left[(x'-w')^{2}-(x'-z)^{2}\right]}\, \frac{1}{\left[(x'-w')^{2}\, (x-z)^{2} - (x-w)^{2}\, (x'-z)^{2}\right]}  \notag \\ & \Bigg[\frac{(x-z)\cdot (z-w)\, \left[(x'-z)^{2}-(x'-w')^{2}\right]}{2\, (z-w)^{2}\, (z-w')^{2}}  - \frac{(x-z)\cdot (x-w)\,\left[(x'-z)^{2}-(x'-w')^{2}\right] }{4\,(x-w)^{2}\,(z-w')^{2}}\notag \\ & +\frac{(x'-z)\cdot (z-w')\, \left[(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\right]}{2\, (z-w)^{2}\, (z-w')^{2}}-\frac{(x'-z)\cdot (x'-w')\, \left[(x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\right]}{4\, (z-w)^{2}\,(x'-w')^{2}}\Bigg]\times\,\notag \\ & \log{\left(\frac{\vee}{k^{+}}\right)}\times\notag \\ & \Bigg[f^{c'bd'}\,f^{cbd}\, U^{ac'}(w')\,U^{ac}(w)\,\rho^{d'}(x')\rho^{d}(x)- f^{c'bd'}\,f^{acd}\,U^{ac'}(w')\,U^{bc}(z)\,U^{de}(x)\,\rho^{d'}(x')\rho^{e}(x) \notag \\ & -f^{ac'd'}\,f^{cbd}\,U^{ac}(w)\,U^{bc'}(z)\,U^{e'd'}(x')\,\rho^{e'}(x')\,\rho^{d}(x) +N_{c}\,U^{e'd}(x')\,U^{de}(x)\,\rho^{e'}(x')\,\rho^{e}(x)\Bigg] \label{eq:B:G2I2e}
\end{align}

\paragraph{Row I, second part, $d^3N_{2f}$.}
\noindent Terms $\propto\log\frac{k^+}{\Lambda}$: none.

\noindent Terms $\propto\log\frac{\vee}{k^+}$:
\begin{align}
    \frac{d^{3}N_{2f\,\rm NLO}^{\rm}}{d^{3}k}  & =  \frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\, \frac{g^{4}\,d_{\perp}}{32\pi^{5}}\,\log{\left(\frac{\vee}{k^{+}}\right)}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\int_{z,w,w^{\prime},x,x'} e^{-ik(w^{\prime}-w)}\,\frac{\left[(x'-w')^{2}\, (x-w)^{2} - (x-z)^{2}\, (x'-z)^{2}\right]}{(z-w)^{2}\, (z-w')^{2}\,\left[(x-z)^{2} - (x-w)^{2}\right]\, \left[(x'-z)^{2} - (x'-w')^{2}\right]}\notag \\ &   \left[f^{c'bd'}\, U^{ac'}(w')\,\rho^{d'}(x')-f^{ac'd'}\, U^{bc'}(z)U^{e'd'}(x')\, \rho^{e'}(x')\right]\times\, \left[f^{cbd}\, U^{ac}(w)\,\rho^{d}(x) - f^{acd}\, U^{bc}(z)\,U^{de}(x)\, \rho^{e}(x)\right]  \notag \\
& - \frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\, \frac{g^{4}\,d_{\perp}}{32\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\int_{z,w,w^{\prime},x,x'} e^{-ik(w^{\prime}-w)}\,\frac{(x-z)^{2}\, (x-w)^{2}}{(z-w)^{2}\, (z-w')^{2}\,\left[(x-z)^{2} - (x-w)^{2}\right]}\,\frac{\left[(x'-z)^{2}-(x'-w')^{2}\right]}{\left[(x'-w')^{2}\, (x-z)^{2} - (x-w)^{2}\, (x'-z)^{2}\right]}\times\notag \\ & \log{\left(\frac{\vee}{k^{+}}\right)}\times\notag \\ & \Bigg[f^{c'bd'}\,f^{cbd}\, U^{ac'}(w')\,U^{ac}(w)\,\rho^{d'}(x')\rho^{d}(x)- f^{c'bd'}\,f^{acd}\,U^{ac'}(w')\,U^{bc}(z)\,U^{de}(x)\,\rho^{d'}(x')\rho^{e}(x) \notag \\ & -f^{ac'd'}\,f^{cbd}\,U^{ac}(w)\,U^{bc'}(z)\,U^{e'd'}(x')\,\rho^{e'}(x')\,\rho^{d}(x) +N_{c}\,U^{e'd}(x')\,U^{de}(x)\,\rho^{e'}(x')\,\rho^{e}(x)\Bigg]  \notag \\
& + \frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\, \frac{g^{4}\,d_{\perp}}{32\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\int_{z,w,w^{\prime},x,x'} e^{-ik(w^{\prime}-w)}\,\frac{(x'-w')^{2}\, (x'-z)^{2}}{(z-w)^{2}\, (z-w')^{2}}\notag \\ & \frac{\left[(x-z)^{2} - (x-w)^{2}\right]}{\left[(x'-z)^{2} - (x'-w')^{2}\right]\, \left[(x'-w')^{2}\, (x - z)^{2} - (x-w)^{2}\,(x'-z)^{2}\right]}\times\, \log{\left(\frac{\vee}{k^{+}}\right)}\times \notag \\ &  \Bigg[f^{c'bd'}\,f^{cbd}\, U^{ac'}(w')\,U^{ac}(w)\,\rho^{d'}(x')\rho^{d}(x)- f^{c'bd'}\,f^{acd}\,U^{ac'}(w')\,U^{bc}(z)\,U^{de}(x)\,\rho^{d'}(x')\rho^{e}(x) \notag \\ & -f^{ac'd'}\,f^{cbd}\,U^{ac}(w)\,U^{bc'}(z)\,U^{e'd'}(x')\,\rho^{e'}(x')\,\rho^{d}(x) +N_{c}\,U^{e'd}(x')\,U^{de}(x)\,\rho^{e'}(x')\,\rho^{e}(x)\Bigg] \label{eq:B:G2I2f}
\end{align}

\subsection{Row II}
Only four $\rho$\textquotesingle s: Sec.~\ref{sec:four} (with the same coefficient for both logarithms).
\subsection{Row III}
Only four $\rho$\textquotesingle s: Sec.~\ref{sec:four} (with the same coefficient for both logarithms).
\subsection{Row IV}
\paragraph{Row IV.}
\textbf{BFKL terms in line 1.} Line 1 is a $\xi$ integral down to $\xi=\frac{k^+}{\vee}$; it contains $\log\frac{\vee}{k^+}$ terms that are brought out by exact identities (Sec.~\ref{sec:exact}):
\begin{itemize}
\item[(i)] line 2: $\log(\frac{\vee}{k^+}-1)=\log\frac{\vee}{k^+}+\log(1-\frac{k^+}{\vee})$; its $\log\frac{\vee}{k^+}$ part is the first line below;
\item[(ii)] line 1, the subtraction in $\frac{\xi}{(1-\xi)_+}$: $-g(1)\log\frac{\vee}{k^+}$, Eq.~\eqref{eq:I2xi}, exactly minus (i), poles included (second line, \new{blue});
\item[(iii)] line 1, the $\log(y'-y)^2$ term of $\frac{1-\xi}{\xi}$ at $y'=y$: $\log\frac{\vee}{k^+}H_4$, Eq.~\eqref{eq:I4} (third line, \new{blue}).
\end{itemize}
(i) and (ii) cancel: the BFKL term of this row is $\log\frac{\vee}{k^+}H_4$. Its $\log\frac{k^+}{\Lambda}$ term is as you have it.
\noindent Terms $\propto\log\frac{k^+}{\Lambda}$:
\begin{align}
    \frac{d^{3}N_{\rm NLO}^{\rm}}{d^{3}k}  & =    +\, \frac{g^{2}}{4\pi^{2}}\, \frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{2}}{2\pi^{2}}\, \frac{N_{c}}{k^{+}}\,\log{\left(\frac{k^{+}}{\Lambda}\right)}\,\int_{x,y,x',y'}\,e^{-ik\cdot (y'-y)}\,\frac{(x-y)\cdot (x'-y')}{(x-y)^{2}\,(x'-y')^{2}}\,\bigg[-\frac{d_{\perp}}{\epsilon}-d_{\perp}\,\gamma_{E}\notag \\ & -\frac{d_{\perp}}{2}\,\log{\left(\frac{(y'-y)^{2}\,\mu^{2}_{\overline{MS}}}{4}\right)}-1\bigg]\,\left[U^{e'c}(y') - U^{e'c}(x')\right] \times\, \left[U^{ec}(y) - U^{ce}(x)\right]\, \rho^{e'}(x') \rho^{e}(x) \label{eq:J:G2IV}
\end{align}

\noindent Terms $\propto\log\frac{\vee}{k^+}$:
\begin{align}
    \frac{d^{3}N_{\rm NLO}^{\rm}}{d^{3}k}  & =    +\, \frac{g^{2}}{4\pi^{2}}\, \frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{2}}{2\pi^{2}}\, \frac{N_{c}}{k^{+}}\,\log{\left(\frac{\vee}{k^{+}}\right)}\,\int_{x,y,x',y'}\,e^{-ik\cdot (y'-y)}\,\frac{(x-y)\cdot (x'-y')}{(x-y)^{2}\,(x'-y')^{2}}\,\bigg[-\frac{d_{\perp}}{\epsilon}-d_{\perp}\,\gamma_{E}\notag \\ & -\frac{d_{\perp}}{2}\,\log{\left(\frac{(y'-y)^{2}\,\mu^{2}_{\overline{MS}}}{4}\right)}-1\bigg]\,\left[U^{e'c}(y') - U^{e'c}(x')\right] \times\, \left[U^{ec}(y) - U^{ce}(x)\right]\, \rho^{e'}(x') \rho^{e}(x) \notag \\
& \nc  -\, \frac{g^{2}}{4\pi^{2}}\, \frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{2}}{2\pi^{2}}\, \frac{N_{c}}{k^{+}}\,\log{\left(\frac{\vee}{k^{+}}\right)}\,\int_{x,y,x',y'}\,e^{-ik\cdot (y'-y)}\,\frac{(x-y)\cdot (x'-y')}{(x-y)^{2}\,(x'-y')^{2}}\,\bigg[-\frac{d_{\perp}}{\epsilon}-d_{\perp}\,\gamma_{E}\notag \\ & \nc  -\frac{d_{\perp}}{2}\,\log{\left(\frac{(y'-y)^{2}\,\mu^{2}_{\overline{MS}}}{4}\right)}-1\bigg]\,\left[U^{e'c}(y') - U^{e'c}(x')\right] \times\, \left[U^{ec}(y) - U^{ce}(x)\right]\, \rho^{e'}(x') \rho^{e}(x) \notag \\
& \nc  \frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}\,N_{c}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\log{\left(\frac{\vee}{k^{+}}\right)}\,\int_{x,x',z,w,w'}\,e^{-ik\cdot (w'-w)}\,\frac{(w-z)\cdot(w'-z)}{(w-z)^{2}\,(w'-z)^{2}}\,\frac{(x-z)\cdot(x'-z)}{(x-z)^{2}\,(x'-z)^{2}}\notag \\ & \nc  \left[U^{e'c}(z) - U^{e'c}(x')\right] \times\, \left[U^{ec}(z) - U^{ce}(x)\right]\, \rho^{e'}(x') \rho^{e}(x) \label{eq:B:G2IV}
\end{align}

\section{Group III (with c.c.)}\label{sec:LLgIII}
\subsection{Row I}
\paragraph{Row I.}
\noindent Terms $\propto\log\frac{k^+}{\Lambda}$:
\begin{align}
     \frac{d^{3}N_{\rm NLO}^{\rm}}{d^{3}k}  & =   \frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\, \int_{x,y,x',z,w,w^{\prime}} e^{-ik(w^{\prime}-w)}\, \Bigg\{\notag \\
& -\frac{ig^{4}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\log{\left(\frac{k^{+}}{\Lambda}\right)}\,\frac{(x'-w')^{i}(y-z)^{j}}{(x'-w')^{2}\,(y-z)^{2}}\,\left[\frac{(x-w)^{i}\,(z-w)^{j}}{(z-w)^{2}\,(x-w)^{2}}+\frac{(x-z)^{j}\,(x-w)^{i}}{2\,(x-z)^{2}\, (x-w)^{2}}\right]\Bigg\}\times \notag \\ &  \Bigg[U^{ab'}(x')\,U^{c'd'}(y)\,U^{c'b}(z)\,\rho^{b'}(x')\rho^{d'}(y)-U^{bd'}(z)\,U^{d'e'}(y)U^{ac'}(w')\,\rho^{c'}(x')\rho^{e'}(y)\Bigg]\times\notag \\ & \Bigg[f^{cbd}\,U^{ac}(w)\,\rho^{d}(x)-f^{acd}\, U^{bc}(z)\,U^{de}(x)\,\rho^{e}(x)\Bigg] \label{eq:J:G3I}
\end{align}

\noindent Terms $\propto\log\frac{\vee}{k^+}$:
\begin{align}
     \frac{d^{3}N_{\rm NLO}^{\rm}}{d^{3}k}  & =   \frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\, \int_{x,y,x',z,w,w^{\prime}} e^{-ik(w^{\prime}-w)}\, \Bigg\{\notag \\
& -\,\frac{ig^{4}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\frac{(x'-w')^{i}(y-z)^{j}}{(x'-w')^{2}\,(y-z)^{2}}\times \log{\left(\frac{\vee}{k^{+}}\right)}\times \Bigg[\frac{\delta^{ij}}{2\,(z-w)^{2}}+\frac{(x-z)^{j}\,(z-w)^{i}}{(z-w)^{2}\,(x-z)^{2}}\notag \\ & -\frac{(x-z)^{j}\,(x-w)^{i}}{(x-w)^{2}\,(x-z)^{2}}-\frac{(x-w)^{i}\,(z-w)^{j}}{(z-w)^{2}\,(x-w)^{2}}\Bigg] \notag \\
& +\,\frac{ig^{4}}{16\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\log{\left(\frac{\vee}{k^{+}}\right)}\, \frac{(x'-w')\cdot (y-z)}{(x'-w')^{2}\,(y-z)^{2}\,(z-w)^{2}} \notag \\
& -\frac{ig^{4}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\log{\left(\frac{\vee}{k^{+}}\right)}\,\frac{(x'-w')^{i}(y-z)^{j}}{(x'-w')^{2}\,(y-z)^{2}}\,\left[\frac{(x-w)^{i}\,(z-w)^{j}}{(z-w)^{2}\,(x-w)^{2}}+\frac{(x-z)^{j}\,(x-w)^{i}}{2\,(x-z)^{2}\, (x-w)^{2}}\right]\Bigg\}\times \notag \\ &  \Bigg[U^{ab'}(x')\,U^{c'd'}(y)\,U^{c'b}(z)\,\rho^{b'}(x')\rho^{d'}(y)-U^{bd'}(z)\,U^{d'e'}(y)U^{ac'}(w')\,\rho^{c'}(x')\rho^{e'}(y)\Bigg]\times\notag \\ & \Bigg[f^{cbd}\,U^{ac}(w)\,\rho^{d}(x)-f^{acd}\, U^{bc}(z)\,U^{de}(x)\,\rho^{e}(x)\Bigg] \label{eq:B:G3I}
\end{align}

\subsection{Row II}
\paragraph{Row II.}
\noindent Terms $\propto\log\frac{k^+}{\Lambda}$:
\begin{align}
     \frac{d^{3}N_{\rm NLO}^{\rm}}{d^{3}k}  & =   \frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\, \int_{x,y,x',z,w,w^{\prime}} e^{-ik(w^{\prime}-w)}\, \Bigg\{\notag \\
& -\frac{ig^{4}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\log{\left(\frac{k^{+}}{\Lambda}\right)}\,\frac{(x'-w')^{i}(y-z)^{j}}{(x'-w')^{2}\,(y-z)^{2}}\,\left[\frac{(x-w)^{i}\,(z-w)^{j}}{(z-w)^{2}\,(x-w)^{2}}+\frac{(x-z)^{j}\,(x-w)^{i}}{2\,(x-z)^{2}\, (x-w)^{2}}\right]\Bigg\}\times \notag \\ & \Bigg[U^{c'd'}(y)\,U^{ab'}(x')\,U^{bc'}(z)\,\rho^{d'}(y)\rho^{b'}(x')-U^{ab'}(x')\,\rho^{b}(y)\rho^{b'}(x')\Bigg]\times\Bigg[f^{cbd}\,U^{ac}(w)\,\rho^{d}(x)-f^{acd}\, U^{bc}(z)\,U^{de}(x)\,\rho^{e}(x)\Bigg] \label{eq:J:G3II}
\end{align}

\noindent Terms $\propto\log\frac{\vee}{k^+}$:
\begin{align}
     \frac{d^{3}N_{\rm NLO}^{\rm}}{d^{3}k}  & =   \frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\, \int_{x,y,x',z,w,w^{\prime}} e^{-ik(w^{\prime}-w)}\, \Bigg\{\notag \\
& -\,\frac{ig^{4}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\frac{(x'-w')^{i}(y-z)^{j}}{(x'-w')^{2}\,(y-z)^{2}}\times \log{\left(\frac{\vee}{k^{+}}\right)}\times \Bigg[\frac{\delta^{ij}}{2\,(z-w)^{2}}+\frac{(x-z)^{j}\,(z-w)^{i}}{(z-w)^{2}\,(x-z)^{2}}\notag \\ & -\frac{(x-w)^{i}\,(z-w)^{j}}{(z-w)^{2}\,(x-w)^{2}}-\frac{(x-z)^{j}\,(x-w)^{i}}{(x-z)^{2}\,(x-w)^{2}}\Bigg] \notag \\
& +\,\frac{ig^{4}}{16\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\log{\left(\frac{\vee}{k^{+}}\right)}\, \frac{(x'-w')\cdot (y-z)}{(x'-w')^{2}\,(y-z)^{2}\,(z-w)^{2}} \notag \\
& -\frac{ig^{4}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\log{\left(\frac{\vee}{k^{+}}\right)}\,\frac{(x'-w')^{i}(y-z)^{j}}{(x'-w')^{2}\,(y-z)^{2}}\,\left[\frac{(x-w)^{i}\,(z-w)^{j}}{(z-w)^{2}\,(x-w)^{2}}+\frac{(x-z)^{j}\,(x-w)^{i}}{2\,(x-z)^{2}\, (x-w)^{2}}\right]\Bigg\}\times \notag \\ & \Bigg[U^{c'd'}(y)\,U^{ab'}(x')\,U^{bc'}(z)\,\rho^{d'}(y)\rho^{b'}(x')-U^{ab'}(x')\,\rho^{b}(y)\rho^{b'}(x')\Bigg]\times\Bigg[f^{cbd}\,U^{ac}(w)\,\rho^{d}(x)-f^{acd}\, U^{bc}(z)\,U^{de}(x)\,\rho^{e}(x)\Bigg] \label{eq:B:G3II}
\end{align}

\subsection{Row III}
\paragraph{Row III, first part.}
\textbf{BFKL terms in line 1.} Line 1 is a $p^+$ integral up to $\vee-k^+$; exact identities bring out (Sec.~\ref{sec:exact}):
\begin{itemize}
\item[(i)] line 2: the $\log\frac{\vee}{k^+}$ part of $\log(\frac{\vee}{k^+}-1)$ (first line below);
\item[(ii)] line 1, the subtraction of $\left[\frac{1}{p^+}\right]_+$: $-f(0)\log\frac{\vee}{k^+}$, Eq.~\eqref{eq:I2}, exactly minus (i) (\new{blue});
\item[(iii)] line 1, the $-\frac{1}{k^+}\delta_{im}\delta_{jk'}$ term with $G(y')\to G(z)$: the term (B), Eq.~\eqref{eq:I3} (\new{blue}).
\end{itemize}
(i) and (ii) cancel, so the BFKL term of this part is (B). Its $\log\frac{k^+}{\Lambda}$ term is as you have it.
\noindent Terms $\propto\log\frac{k^+}{\Lambda}$:
\begin{align}
     \frac{d^{3}N_{1\,\rm NLO}}{d^{3}k} & =   -\,\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\, \frac{ig^{4}\,f^{c'd'a}}{16\pi^{5}}\, \frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\log{\left(\frac{k^{+}}{\Lambda}\right)}\, \int_{w,x',y',z,y,x}\, e^{-ik\cdot (y'-w)}\,\frac{(y'-z)\cdot (x-z)\,(x'-y')\cdot (y-w)}{(y'-z)^{2}\,(x'-y')^{2}\,(y-w)^{2}\,(x-z)^{2}}\notag \\ &  \left[U^{e'c'}(y')\,U^{d'b}(z)\,\rho^{e'}(x')-U^{bd'}(z)\,U^{c'e'}(x')\,\rho^{e'}(x')\right]\times  \left[U^{ac}(w)\, \{\rho^{c}(y),\rho^{b}(x)\}-U^{bc}(z)\,U^{ad}(y)\,U^{ce}(x)\,\{\rho^{d}(y),\rho^{e}(x)\}\right] \label{eq:J:G3III1}
\end{align}

\noindent Terms $\propto\log\frac{\vee}{k^+}$:
\begin{align}
     \frac{d^{3}N_{1\,\rm NLO}}{d^{3}k} & =   -\,\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\, \frac{ig^{4}\,f^{c'd'a}}{16\pi^{5}}\, \frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\log{\left(\frac{\vee}{k^{+}}\right)}\, \int_{w,x',y',z,y,x}\, e^{-ik\cdot (y'-w)}\,\frac{(y'-z)\cdot (x-z)\,(x'-y')\cdot (y-w)}{(y'-z)^{2}\,(x'-y')^{2}\,(y-w)^{2}\,(x-z)^{2}}\notag \\ &  \left[U^{e'c'}(y')\,U^{d'b}(z)\,\rho^{e'}(x')-U^{bd'}(z)\,U^{c'e'}(x')\,\rho^{e'}(x')\right]\times  \left[U^{ac}(w)\, \{\rho^{c}(y),\rho^{b}(x)\}-U^{bc}(z)\,U^{ad}(y)\,U^{ce}(x)\,\{\rho^{d}(y),\rho^{e}(x)\}\right] \notag \\
& \nc  +\,\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\, \frac{ig^{4}\,f^{c'd'a}}{16\pi^{5}}\, \frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\log{\left(\frac{\vee}{k^{+}}\right)}\, \int_{w,x',y',z,y,x}\, e^{-ik\cdot (y'-w)}\,\frac{(y'-z)\cdot (x-z)\,(x'-y')\cdot (y-w)}{(y'-z)^{2}\,(x'-y')^{2}\,(y-w)^{2}\,(x-z)^{2}}\notag \\ & \nc   \left[U^{e'c'}(y')\,U^{d'b}(z)\,\rho^{e'}(x')-U^{bd'}(z)\,U^{c'e'}(x')\,\rho^{e'}(x')\right]\times  \left[U^{ac}(w)\, \{\rho^{c}(y),\rho^{b}(x)\}-U^{bc}(z)\,U^{ad}(y)\,U^{ce}(x)\,\{\rho^{d}(y),\rho^{e}(x)\}\right] \notag \\
& \nc  -\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\, \frac{ig^{4}\,f^{c'd'a}}{16\pi^{5}}\, \frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\log{\left(\frac{\vee}{k^{+}}\right)}\int_{w,x',w',z,y,x} e^{-ik\cdot (w'-w)}\,\frac{(w'-z)\cdot (y-w)\,(x'-z)\cdot (x-z)}{(w'-z)^{2}\,(y-w)^{2}\,(x'-z)^{2}\,(x-z)^{2}}\notag \\
& \nc  \left[U^{e'c'}(z)\,U^{d'b}(z)\,\rho^{e'}(x')-U^{bd'}(z)\,U^{c'e'}(x')\,\rho^{e'}(x')\right]\notag \\
& \nc  \times\left[U^{ac}(w)\, \{\rho^{c}(y),\rho^{b}(x)\}-U^{bc}(z)\,U^{ad}(y)\,U^{ce}(x)\,\{\rho^{d}(y),\rho^{e}(x)\}\right] \label{eq:B:G3III1}
\end{align}

\paragraph{Row III, second part.}
(i), (ii), (iii) as in the first part; (B) comes from the $-\frac{p^+}{k^{+2}}$ term, Eqs.~\eqref{eq:I3}, \eqref{eq:I5}. \textbf{C5}: the measure is $\int_{w,x',y',z,x}$ (the row has $\rho(x')\rho(x)$; there is no $y$ and no $w'$).
\noindent Terms $\propto\log\frac{k^+}{\Lambda}$:
\begin{align}
 \frac{d^{3}N_{2\,\rm NLO}}{d^{3}k} & =    -\, \frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\log{\left(\frac{k^{+}}{\Lambda}\right)}\,\old{\int_{w,y',z,y,x}}\,\new{\int_{w,x',y',z,x}}\, e^{-ik\cdot (y'-w)}\,\bigg[\frac{(x-w)\cdot (x'-y')\,(z-w)\cdot (y'-z)}{(z-w)^{2}\,(x-w)^{2}\,(y'-z)^{2}\,(x'-y')^{2}}\notag \\ & +\frac{(x-z)\cdot (y'-z)\,(x-w)\cdot (x'-y')}{2\,(x-z)^{2}\,(x-w)^{2}\,(y'-z)^{2}\,(x'-y')^{2}}\bigg]\, \left[f^{c'd'a}\,U^{e'c'}(y')\,U^{d'b}(z)\,\rho^{e'}(x')-f^{c'd'a}\, 
    U^{bd'}(z)\,U^{c'e'}(x')\,\rho^{e'}(x')\right]\times\notag \\ &  \left[f^{cbd}\,U^{ac}(w)\,\rho^{d}(x) -f^{acd}\,U^{bc}(z)\,U^{de}(x)\,\rho^{e}(x)\right] \label{eq:J:G3III2}
\end{align}

\noindent Terms $\propto\log\frac{\vee}{k^+}$:
\begin{align}
 \frac{d^{3}N_{2\,\rm NLO}}{d^{3}k} & =    -\, \frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\log{\left(\frac{\vee}{k^{+}}\right)}\,\old{\int_{w,y',z,y,x}}\,\new{\int_{w,x',y',z,x}}\, e^{-ik\cdot (y'-w)}\,\bigg[\frac{(x-w)\cdot (x'-y')\,(z-w)\cdot (y'-z)}{(z-w)^{2}\,(x-w)^{2}\,(y'-z)^{2}\,(x'-y')^{2}}\notag \\ & +\frac{(x-z)\cdot (y'-z)\,(x-w)\cdot (x'-y')}{2\,(x-z)^{2}\,(x-w)^{2}\,(y'-z)^{2}\,(x'-y')^{2}}\bigg]\, \left[f^{c'd'a}\,U^{e'c'}(y')\,U^{d'b}(z)\,\rho^{e'}(x')-f^{c'd'a}\, 
    U^{bd'}(z)\,U^{c'e'}(x')\,\rho^{e'}(x')\right]\times\notag \\ &  \left[f^{cbd}\,U^{ac}(w)\,\rho^{d}(x) -f^{acd}\,U^{bc}(z)\,U^{de}(x)\,\rho^{e}(x)\right] \notag \\
& \nc  +\, \frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\log{\left(\frac{\vee}{k^{+}}\right)}\,\old{\int_{w,y',z,y,x}}\,\new{\int_{w,x',y',z,x}}\, e^{-ik\cdot (y'-w)}\,\bigg[\frac{(x-w)\cdot (x'-y')\,(z-w)\cdot (y'-z)}{(z-w)^{2}\,(x-w)^{2}\,(y'-z)^{2}\,(x'-y')^{2}}\notag \\ & \nc  +\frac{(x-z)\cdot (y'-z)\,(x-w)\cdot (x'-y')}{2\,(x-z)^{2}\,(x-w)^{2}\,(y'-z)^{2}\,(x'-y')^{2}}\bigg]\, \left[f^{c'd'a}\,U^{e'c'}(y')\,U^{d'b}(z)\,\rho^{e'}(x')-f^{c'd'a}\, 
    U^{bd'}(z)\,U^{c'e'}(x')\,\rho^{e'}(x')\right]\times\notag \\ & \nc   \left[f^{cbd}\,U^{ac}(w)\,\rho^{d}(x) -f^{acd}\,U^{bc}(z)\,U^{de}(x)\,\rho^{e}(x)\right] \notag \\
& \nc  -\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{8\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\log{\left(\frac{\vee}{k^{+}}\right)}\int_{w,x',w',z,x} e^{-ik\cdot (w'-w)}\,\frac{(x'-z)\cdot (x-z)}{(x'-z)^{2}\,(x-z)^{2}}\notag \\
& \nc  \times\bigg[\frac{(w'-z)\cdot (z-w)}{(w'-z)^{2}\,(z-w)^{2}}-\frac{(w'-z)\cdot (x-w)}{2\,(w'-z)^{2}\,(x-w)^{2}}\bigg]\notag \\
& \nc  \left[f^{c'd'a}\,U^{e'c'}(z)\,U^{d'b}(z)\,\rho^{e'}(x')-f^{c'd'a}\, U^{bd'}(z)\,U^{c'e'}(x')\,\rho^{e'}(x')\right]\notag \\
& \nc  \times\left[f^{cbd}\,U^{ac}(w)\,\rho^{d}(x) -f^{acd}\,U^{bc}(z)\,U^{de}(x)\,\rho^{e}(x)\right] \label{eq:B:G3III2}
\end{align}

\subsection{Row IV}
Only four $\rho$\textquotesingle s: Sec.~\ref{sec:four} (with the same coefficient for both logarithms).
\subsection{Row V}
\paragraph{Row V.}
(i), (ii), (iii) as in Row III: (i) and (ii) cancel, and (B) comes from the $-\frac{1}{k^+}\delta_{im}\delta_{jk'}$ term.
\noindent Terms $\propto\log\frac{k^+}{\Lambda}$:
\begin{align}
    \frac{d^{3}N_{\rm NLO}}{d^{3}k} & =   -\,\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\, \frac{ig^{4}\,f^{c'ca}}{8\pi^{5}}\, \frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\log{\left(\frac{k^{+}}{\Lambda}\right)} \int_{w,y',z,y,x,x'}\, e^{-ik\cdot (y'-w)}\,\frac{(y'-z)\cdot (x-z)\,(x'-y')\cdot (y-w)}{(y'-z)^{2}\,(x'-y')^{2}\,(y-w)^{2}\,(x-z)^{2}}\notag \\ & \left[U^{e'c'}(y')\,\rho^{e'}(x')-U^{c'e'}(x')\,\rho^{e'}(x')\right]\times  \left[U^{ce}(x)\,U^{ad}(y)\,\rho^{e}(x)\rho^{d}(y)-U^{ad}(w)\,U^{ce}(x)\,\rho^{e}(x)\rho^{d}(y)\right] \label{eq:J:G3V}
\end{align}

\noindent Terms $\propto\log\frac{\vee}{k^+}$:
\begin{align}
    \frac{d^{3}N_{\rm NLO}}{d^{3}k} & =   -\,\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\, \frac{ig^{4}\,f^{c'ca}}{8\pi^{5}}\, \frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\log{\left(\frac{\vee}{k^{+}}\right)} \int_{w,y',z,y,x,x'}\, e^{-ik\cdot (y'-w)}\,\frac{(y'-z)\cdot (x-z)\,(x'-y')\cdot (y-w)}{(y'-z)^{2}\,(x'-y')^{2}\,(y-w)^{2}\,(x-z)^{2}}\notag \\ & \left[U^{e'c'}(y')\,\rho^{e'}(x')-U^{c'e'}(x')\,\rho^{e'}(x')\right]\times  \left[U^{ce}(x)\,U^{ad}(y)\,\rho^{e}(x)\rho^{d}(y)-U^{ad}(w)\,U^{ce}(x)\,\rho^{e}(x)\rho^{d}(y)\right] \notag \\
& \nc  +\,\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\, \frac{ig^{4}\,f^{c'ca}}{8\pi^{5}}\, \frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\log{\left(\frac{\vee}{k^{+}}\right)} \int_{w,y',z,y,x,x'}\, e^{-ik\cdot (y'-w)}\,\frac{(y'-z)\cdot (x-z)\,(x'-y')\cdot (y-w)}{(y'-z)^{2}\,(x'-y')^{2}\,(y-w)^{2}\,(x-z)^{2}}\notag \\ & \nc  \left[U^{e'c'}(y')\,\rho^{e'}(x')-U^{c'e'}(x')\,\rho^{e'}(x')\right]\times  \left[U^{ce}(x)\,U^{ad}(y)\,\rho^{e}(x)\rho^{d}(y)-U^{ad}(w)\,U^{ce}(x)\,\rho^{e}(x)\rho^{d}(y)\right] \notag \\
& \nc  -\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\, \frac{ig^{4}\,f^{c'ca}}{8\pi^{5}}\, \frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\log{\left(\frac{\vee}{k^{+}}\right)}\int_{w,w',z,y,x,x'} e^{-ik\cdot (w'-w)}\,\frac{(w'-z)\cdot (y-w)\,(x'-z)\cdot (x-z)}{(w'-z)^{2}\,(y-w)^{2}\,(x'-z)^{2}\,(x-z)^{2}}\notag \\
& \nc  \left[U^{e'c'}(z)\,\rho^{e'}(x')-U^{c'e'}(x')\,\rho^{e'}(x')\right]\left[U^{ce}(x)\,U^{ad}(y)\,\rho^{e}(x)\rho^{d}(y)-U^{ad}(w)\,U^{ce}(x)\,\rho^{e}(x)\rho^{d}(y)\right] \label{eq:B:G3V}
\end{align}

\subsection{Row VI}
\paragraph{Row VI.}
(i), (ii), (iii) as in Row III: (i) and (ii) cancel, and (B) comes from the $-\frac{1}{k^+}\delta_{im}\delta_{jk'}$ term.
\noindent Terms $\propto\log\frac{k^+}{\Lambda}$:
\begin{align}
    \frac{d^{3}N_{\rm NLO}}{d^{3}k} & =    -\,\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\, \frac{ig^{4}\,f^{c'd'a}}{8\pi^{5}}\, \frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\log{\left(\frac{k^{+}}{\Lambda}\right)}\, \int_{w,y',z,y,x,x'}\, e^{-ik\cdot (y'-w)}\,\frac{(y'-z)\cdot (x-z)\,(x'-y')\cdot (y-w)}{(y'-z)^{2}\,(x'-y')^{2}\,(y-w)^{2}\,(x-z)^{2}}\notag \\ & \left[U^{e'c'}(y')\,U^{d'b}(z)\,\rho^{e'}(x')-U^{bd'}(z)\,U^{c'e'}(x')\,\rho^{e'}(x')\right]\times  \left[U^{bc}(z)\,U^{ad}(y)\,U^{ce}(x)\,\rho^{d}(y)\,\rho^{e}(x)-U^{ad}(y)\,\rho^{d}(y)\,\rho^{b}(x)\right] \label{eq:J:G3VI}
\end{align}

\noindent Terms $\propto\log\frac{\vee}{k^+}$:
\begin{align}
    \frac{d^{3}N_{\rm NLO}}{d^{3}k} & =    -\,\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\, \frac{ig^{4}\,f^{c'd'a}}{8\pi^{5}}\, \frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\log{\left(\frac{\vee}{k^{+}}\right)}\, \int_{w,y',z,y,x,x'}\, e^{-ik\cdot (y'-w)}\,\frac{(y'-z)\cdot (x-z)\,(x'-y')\cdot (y-w)}{(y'-z)^{2}\,(x'-y')^{2}\,(y-w)^{2}\,(x-z)^{2}}\notag \\ & \left[U^{e'c'}(y')\,U^{d'b}(z)\,\rho^{e'}(x')-U^{bd'}(z)\,U^{c'e'}(x')\,\rho^{e'}(x')\right]\times  \left[U^{bc}(z)\,U^{ad}(y)\,U^{ce}(x)\,\rho^{d}(y)\,\rho^{e}(x)-U^{ad}(y)\,\rho^{d}(y)\,\rho^{b}(x)\right] \notag \\
& \nc  +\,\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\, \frac{ig^{4}\,f^{c'd'a}}{8\pi^{5}}\, \frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\log{\left(\frac{\vee}{k^{+}}\right)}\, \int_{w,y',z,y,x,x'}\, e^{-ik\cdot (y'-w)}\,\frac{(y'-z)\cdot (x-z)\,(x'-y')\cdot (y-w)}{(y'-z)^{2}\,(x'-y')^{2}\,(y-w)^{2}\,(x-z)^{2}}\notag \\ & \nc  \left[U^{e'c'}(y')\,U^{d'b}(z)\,\rho^{e'}(x')-U^{bd'}(z)\,U^{c'e'}(x')\,\rho^{e'}(x')\right]\times  \left[U^{bc}(z)\,U^{ad}(y)\,U^{ce}(x)\,\rho^{d}(y)\,\rho^{e}(x)-U^{ad}(y)\,\rho^{d}(y)\,\rho^{b}(x)\right] \notag \\
& \nc  -\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\, \frac{ig^{4}\,f^{c'd'a}}{8\pi^{5}}\, \frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\log{\left(\frac{\vee}{k^{+}}\right)}\int_{w,w',z,y,x,x'} e^{-ik\cdot (w'-w)}\,\frac{(w'-z)\cdot (y-w)\,(x'-z)\cdot (x-z)}{(w'-z)^{2}\,(y-w)^{2}\,(x'-z)^{2}\,(x-z)^{2}}\notag \\
& \nc  \left[U^{e'c'}(z)\,U^{d'b}(z)\,\rho^{e'}(x')-U^{bd'}(z)\,U^{c'e'}(x')\,\rho^{e'}(x')\right]\notag \\
& \nc  \times\left[U^{bc}(z)\,U^{ad}(y)\,U^{ce}(x)\,\rho^{d}(y)\,\rho^{e}(x)-U^{ad}(y)\,\rho^{d}(y)\,\rho^{b}(x)\right] \label{eq:B:G3VI}
\end{align}


\section{Four \texorpdfstring{$\rho$}{rho}'s}\label{sec:four}
Group I Row I, Group II Rows II and III and Group III Row IV have only four $\rho$\textquotesingle s: your \texttt{4rho.tex}. Every four-$\rho$ term carries $\big[\log\frac{k^+}{\Lambda}+\log\frac{\vee}{k^+}\big]$: the same coefficient for both logarithms.
\begin{itemize}
\item The four-$\rho$ terms cancel after symmetrization (Sec.~\ref{sec:files}: $1.3\times10^{-16}$).
\item Their three-$\rho$ remainders, your final forms, enter both three-$\rho$ lists of Sec.~\ref{sec:three}:
\end{itemize}
\begin{align}
    \frac{d^{3}N_{\rm NLO}}{d^{3}k} & = -\,\frac{i}{(2\pi)^{3}}\frac{g^{4}}{8\pi^{3}}\frac{1}{2\pi^{2}} \frac{1}{k^{+}}\,{\color{blue}\left[\log{\left[\frac{k^{+}}{\Lambda}\right]}+\log{\left[\frac{\vee}{k^{+}}\right]}\right]}\,\,\int_{x,y,z,x^{\prime},w,w^{\prime}}e^{-ik(w^{\prime}-w)}\, \Bigg[\notag \\ &  \mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot \mathcal{K}(x-w)\, \mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot \mathcal{K}(y-z)\, f^{a b c} \rho^{d}(x') \rho^{a}(y) \rho^{c}(x)\,U^{d e}(w') U^{e b}(w) \nonumber\\
    &  -\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot \mathcal{K}(x-w)\, \mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot \mathcal{K}(y-z)\, f^{a b c} \rho^{e}(x') \rho^{a}(y) \rho^{c}(x)\, U^{d b}(w) U^{e d}(x')\nonumber\\
    & + \mathcal{K}(x-w)\cdot \mathcal{K}(x'-w')\, \mathcal{K}(x'-z)\cdot \mathcal{K}(y-z)\, f^{a b c} \rho^{c}(x') \rho^{a}(y) \rho^{e}(x)\,U^{d b}(w') U^{e d}(x) \nonumber\\
    & -\,\mathcal{K}(x-w)\cdot \mathcal{K}(x'-w')\, \mathcal{K}(x'-z)\cdot \mathcal{K}(y-z)\, f^{a b c} \rho^{c}(x') \rho^{a}(y) \rho^{e}(x) U^{b d}(w') U^{e d}(w)\Bigg]
\label{eq:foura}
\end{align}
\begin{align}
      \frac{d^{3}N_{\rm NLO}}{d^{3}k} & = \frac{i}{(2\pi)^{3}}\frac{g^{4}}{8\pi^{3}}\frac{1}{2\pi^{2}} \frac{1}{k^{+}}\,{\color{blue}\left[\log{\left[\frac{k^{+}}{\Lambda}\right]}+\log{\left[\frac{\vee}{k^{+}}\right]}\right]}\,\,\int_{x,y,z,x^{\prime},w,w^{\prime}}e^{-ik(w^{\prime}-w)}\, \Bigg[\notag \\ & - \mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot \mathcal{K}(x-w)\, \mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot \mathcal{K}(y-z)\, f^{a b c} \rho^{d}(x') \rho^{h}(y) \rho^{e}(x) U^{d b}(w') U^{e c}(x) U^{a h}(y)\nonumber\\
    &+ \mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot \mathcal{K}(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-z)\cdot \mathcal{K}(y-z)\, f^{a b c} \rho^{d}(x') \rho^{e}(x) \rho^{h}(y) U^{d c}(x') U^{e b}(w) U^{a h}(y)\nonumber\\
    &- \mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot \mathcal{K}(x-w)\,\mathcal{K}(x'-z)\cdot \mathcal{K}(y-z)\, f^{a b c} \rho^{d}(x') \rho^{e}(x) \rho^{h}(y) U^{d c}(x') U^{b e}(x) U^{a h}(y)\nonumber\\
    &+  \mathcal{K}(x'-w')\cdot \mathcal{K}(x-w)\, \mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot \mathcal{K}(y-z)\, f^{a b c} \rho^{e}(x') \rho^{h}(y) \rho^{d}(x) U^{d c}(x) U^{b e}(x') U^{a h}(y) \Bigg]
\label{eq:fourb}
\end{align}
\begin{itemize}
\item Their two-$\rho$ remainders (your ``Two $\rho$ contribution'', with the log bracket restored in the first part and F1, F2) enter both two-$\rho$ sums, Secs.~\ref{sec:two} and \ref{sec:bfkl}.
\end{itemize}

\section{The three-\texorpdfstring{$\rho$}{rho} terms cancel}\label{sec:three}
All terms with three $\rho$\textquotesingle s of Secs.~\ref{sec:LLgI}--\ref{sec:four}, for each logarithm separately. Which terms have three $\rho$\textquotesingle s is decided numerically ($\rho\to2\rho$ multiplies them by 8).
\paragraph{Method.}
\begin{itemize}
\item The charges are integration variables, so only the totally symmetric part of $\rho\rho\rho$ can cancel against other terms. The rest (commutators, $[\rho^a(u),\rho^b(v)]=if^{abc}\rho^c(u)\delta(u-v)$) are two-$\rho$ terms; they are in Secs.~\ref{sec:two} and \ref{sec:bfkl}. The symmetric part is evaluated with classical charges.
\item In every term the integration variables are renamed so that the phase is $e^{-ik\cdot(w'-w)}$, the soft gluon is $z$ and the charges are $x,y,x'$: in Group I Row V part 1 the measured gluon of the amplitude is at $z$ (line 3) or $x$ (line 4); in Group III Rows III, V, VI and in (B) the conjugate one is at $y'$.
\item Each term is summed over the 6 placements of its three charges and, for Groups I and III, its c.c.\ is added (conjugate, $w\leftrightarrow w'$). Group II Row I is self-conjugate as written: its second line is the c.c.\ of the first. The \texttt{4rho.tex} lists are self-conjugate as written.
\item In Group I Row II $d^3N_{2a}$, lines 2 and 3, the $z$ integral is done. For a pointwise test it is written back: $\log\frac{(w-y)^2}{(x-y)^2}=-\frac1\pi\int_z\big[K(w,y,z)-K(x,y,z)\big]$, $K(a,b,z)=\mathcal K(a-z)\cdot\mathcal K(b-z)$.
\item Random points, random SU(3) adjoint Wilson lines with $U^{bc}=U^{cb}$, random charges; three configurations.
\end{itemize}

\end{longtable}
\noindent Largest relative residual over three configurations: final: $1.5\times10^{-16}$; without (B): $3.1\times10^{-16}$.
\paragraph{So:}
\begin{itemize}
\item \textbf{The three-$\rho$ terms $\propto\log\frac{k^+}{\Lambda}$ cancel}, with $\mathcal D$ in place of the edge-kernel term of Group I Row III $d^3N_{3a}$. With the edge kernel they do not.
\item \textbf{The three-$\rho$ terms $\propto\log\frac{\vee}{k^+}$ cancel.} The (B) terms of Group III Rows III(1) and V cancel each other, that of Row VI vanishes by itself after symmetrization; so the three-$\rho$ check does not test (B). (B) is tested by the two-$\rho$ sum, Sec.~\ref{sec:bfkl}.
\item Adding a c.c.\ to the \texttt{4rho.tex} lists would double them: they already contain it.
\item Group I Row IV and Group III Row I cancel each other line by line, and so do several pairs of lines in Group I Row III.
\end{itemize}

\section{The two-$\rho$ terms $\propto\log\frac{k^+}{\Lambda}$: JIMWLK}\label{sec:two}
Number of terms (green / purple): genuine, expanded: 135 / 4; \texttt{4rho.tex} lists: 0 / 30; from reordering three and four $\rho$\textquotesingle s: 208 / 127. No orange term appears.

\subsection{Genuine two-$\rho$ terms of the rows}\label{sec:gen}
These are the two-$\rho$ terms $\propto\log\frac{k^+}{\Lambda}$ of Secs.~\ref{sec:LLgI}--\ref{sec:LLgIII} (Group I Rows II and V, Group II Rows I and IV, Group III Row III part 2), written in a common notation. To compare term by term, every dimensionally regulated bracket is written back as the $z$ integral it comes from (exact in $d_\perp=2-\epsilon$, $\overline{\rm MS}$):
\begin{itemize}
\item Group I Row II: $\frac{2}{\epsilon}+2\gamma+\log\frac{(x-w)^2\mu^2}{4}=-\frac1\pi\int_z\big[K(x,w,z)-K(w,w,z)\big]$;
\item Group I Row V 2b UV: $\frac{2}{\epsilon}+2\gamma_E+\log\frac{(y-z)^2\mu^2}{4}=\frac1\pi\int_x\big[K(z,z,x)-K(y,z,x)\big]$;
\item Group II Row IV: $-\frac{d_\perp}{\epsilon}-d_\perp\gamma_E-\frac{d_\perp}{2}\log\frac{(y'-y)^2\mu^2}{4}-1=\frac1\pi\int_z\big[K(y,y',z)-\frac12K(y,y,z)-\frac12K(y',y',z)\big]+O(\epsilon)$.
\end{itemize}
The terms are expanded, the charges are named $x$ (emission ending at $w$) and $x'$ (ending at $w'$), and the colour algebra is done. Group I and Group III include their c.c.
\begin{itemize}
\item \textbf{Row V 2b: the UV term and the finite counterterm cancel each other.} Both have the kernel $\mathcal K(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal K(y-z)\,[K(z,z,x)-K(y,z,x)]$ and the colour $N_c[U(y)-U(z)]$.
\begin{itemize}
\item UV: $+\frac{g^4N_c}{8\pi^4}\cdot\frac1\pi\int_x$.
\item Counterterm: $-\frac{g^4}{8\pi^5}N_c$, pointwise in $x$.
\end{itemize}
So the $\log\frac{k^+}{\Lambda}$ part of Row V 2b is just its two endpoint terms. This is the same as before the UV subtraction.
\item Row V 2b UV is taken with the coefficient $2\log\frac{k^+}{\Lambda}$ (E1).
\end{itemize}

\subsection{From four $\rho$'s: the two-$\rho$ lists of \texttt{4rho.tex} }
These are the two-$\rho$ terms of \texttt{4rho.tex} (``Two $\rho$ contribution'', $dN^{(1)}$, $dN^{(2)}$ with c.c.), with the two fixes found earlier (both still needed in this \texttt{4rho.tex}):
\begin{itemize}
\item \textbf{F1:} in the first part, drop \old{$-8N_c\,\mathcal K(x'-w')\cdot\mathcal K(x'-w)\,K(y,x',z)$};
\item \textbf{F2:} in the second part, $dN^{(1)}$, term 2 has one index four times. The correct term is
\begin{equation*}\new{3f^{abc}f^{deg}\rho^{b'}(x')\rho^c(y)U^{ag}(y)U^{db'}(w')U^{eb}(z)}.\end{equation*}
\end{itemize}

\subsection{From reordering the three and four $\rho$\textquotesingle s}
The commutator terms of the three-$\rho$ terms of Sec.~\ref{sec:three} and of the four-$\rho$ terms. They are your lists of \texttt{Evolution\_terms.tex}, Sec.~2, which were checked row by row against these rows (0 differing kernel monomials in every row, \texttt{Final\_evolution\_check.pdf}), here with $\log\frac{\vee}{\Lambda}\to\new{\log\frac{k^+}{\Lambda}}$ and coloured.

&{\color{JIM} -\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(w-z)\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{ad}(w)\,U^{bl}(z)\,U^{ce}(x')\,\rho^{l}(x)\,\rho^{h}(x')} \notag\\
&{\color{JIM} +N_{c}\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(w-z)\,U^{ab}(x)\,U^{ac}(x')\,\rho^{b}(x)\,\rho^{c}(x')} \notag\\
&{\color{JIM} -N_{c}\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(w-z)\,U^{ab}(z)\,U^{bc}(x')\,\rho^{a}(x)\,\rho^{c}(x')} \notag\\

&{\color{JIM} +\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(w'-z)\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{ae}(w')\,U^{bl}(z)\,U^{cd}(x')\,\rho^{h}(x')\,\rho^{l}(x)} \notag\\
&{\color{JIM} +N_{c}\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(w'-z)\,U^{ab}(x)\,U^{ac}(x')\,\rho^{c}(x')\,\rho^{b}(x)} \notag\\
&{\color{JIM} -N_{c}\,\mathcal{K}(x'-w)\cdot\mathcal{K}(x'-w')\,\mathcal{K}(x-z)\cdot\mathcal{K}(w'-z)\,U^{ab}(z)\,U^{ac}(x')\,\rho^{c}(x')\,\rho^{b}(x)}\Bigg] \end{align}


\subsection{The sum}

\begin{tabular}{@{}llrr@{}}\toprule

$\mathcal K(x-w)\cdot\mathcal K(x'-w')\;K(x,x',z)$ & {\color{CC}charge--charge} & 13 & 0\\
$\mathcal K(x-w)\cdot\mathcal K(x'-w')\;K(w,w,z)$ & {\color{JIM}JIMWLK only} & 10 & 0\\
$\mathcal K(x-w)\cdot\mathcal K(x'-w')\;K(w',w',z)$ & {\color{JIM}JIMWLK only} & 10 & 0\\

\noindent Without the \texttt{4rho.tex} lists, 18 of 152 colour structures would differ (in $\mathcal K(x-w)\cdot\mathcal K(x'-w')\;K(x,x,z)$, $\mathcal K(x-w)\cdot\mathcal K(x'-w')\;K(x',x',z)$, $\mathcal K(x-w)\cdot\mathcal K(x'-w')\;K(x,x',z)$). \section{The two-$\rho$ terms $\propto\log\frac{\vee}{k^+}$: BFKL}\label{sec:bfkl}
The coefficient of $\log\frac{\vee}{k^+}$ of every row is its $\log\frac{\vee}{k^+}$ list of Secs.~\ref{sec:LLgI}--\ref{sec:LLgIII}.
\begin{itemize}
\item It is zero for Group I Rows II (part 1) and V, and for $\mathcal D$.
\item For the four-$\rho$ terms, their three-$\rho$ remainders and the \texttt{4rho.tex} lists it equals their $\log\frac{k^+}{\Lambda}$ terms.
\item Group III Rows III, V, VI: the terms (B); Group II Row IV: $H_4$.
\end{itemize}
The two-$\rho$ terms (genuine and from reordering the three- and four-$\rho$ terms), row by row (orange / purple: 168 / 162):
\paragraph{Group I, Row II (three $\rho$;).}
\begin{align}

\section{The targets}\label{sec:targets}
In both formulas $\delta Y$ is the evolution interval: $\delta Y=\log\frac{k^+}{\Lambda}$ for JIMWLK and $\delta Y=\log\frac{\vee}{k^+}$ for BFKL. With $\langle\rho^a\rho^b\rangle=\delta^{ab}\langle\rho^f\rho^f\rangle/(N_c^2-1)$ and $\int_{w,w'}e^{-ik\cdot(w'-w)}$, $\mathcal N\,\delta Y\leftrightarrow\frac{\alpha_s^2\,\delta Y}{8\pi^6(N_c^2-1)}$.
\subsection{JIMWLK$\otimes$LO: your formula, corrected (M2)}
The JIMWLK formula of your last message is the one checked before (your App.~F): $M=(U^\dagger(x)-U^\dagger(t))(U(y)-U(t))$ in every term of $dN^{(1)}$, $dN^{(3)}$, $M=(U^\dagger(y)-U^\dagger(t))(U(x)-U(t))$ in every term of $dN^{(2)}$, $dN^{(4)}$, and $dN^{(5)}$ with an overall minus sign. (In your message the signs in front of lines 2--4 of $dN^{(5)}$ came through as bullets; they are read as in your App.~F, $-,-,+$, as below.) As sent, it differs from JIMWLK$\otimes$LO, i.e.\ from the two-$\rho$ sum of Sec.~\ref{sec:two}, by 0.22--0.47 (relative; six configurations, symmetric and general $U$). Corrected as below, it agrees to $2.1\times10^{-15}$.
With $P\equiv\frac{\alpha_s^2\delta Y}{8\pi^6(N_c^2-1)}$ and $\int\equiv\int_{x,y,t}\mathcal K(x-w)\cdot\mathcal K(y-w')\langle\rho^f(x)\rho^f(y)\rangle$, the rule is: in a term $\mathcal K(u,v,t)\,{\rm Tr}\{T^eU^\dagger(v)U(u)T^e\,M\}$,
\begin{equation*}
M=\new{(U^\dagger(u)-U^\dagger(t))(U(v)-U(t))},
\end{equation*}
i.e.\ $M$ sits at the two points the colour charges act on. As sent, $M$ was always \old{$(U^\dagger(x)-U^\dagger(t))(U(y)-U(t))$} in $dN^{(1)}$, $dN^{(3)}$ and \old{$(U^\dagger(y)-U^\dagger(t))(U(x)-U(t))$} in $dN^{(2)}$, $dN^{(4)}$. The corrected formula:
\begin{align}
\frac{dN^{(1)}}{d^2wd^2w'dy}={}&P\int\Big[\notag\\&\qquad\mathcal K(y,x,t){\rm Tr}\{T^eU^\dagger(x)U(y)T^e\new{(U^\dagger(y)-U^\dagger(t))(U(x)-U(t))}\}\notag\\&\qquad-\mathcal K(w',x,t){\rm Tr}\{T^eU^\dagger(x)U(w')T^e\new{(U^\dagger(w')-U^\dagger(t))(U(x)-U(t))}\}\notag\\
&\qquad-\mathcal K(y,w,t){\rm Tr}\{T^eU^\dagger(w)U(y)T^e\new{(U^\dagger(y)-U^\dagger(t))(U(w)-U(t))}\}\notag\\&\qquad+\mathcal K(w',w,t){\rm Tr}\{T^eU^\dagger(w)U(w')T^e\new{(U^\dagger(w')-U^\dagger(t))(U(w)-U(t))}\}\Big],\notag\\
\frac{dN^{(2)}}{d^2wd^2w'dy}={}&P\int\Big[\notag\\&\qquad\mathcal K(x,y,t){\rm Tr}\{T^eU^\dagger(x)U(y)T^e(U^\dagger(y)-U^\dagger(t))(U(x)-U(t))\}\notag\\&\qquad-\mathcal K(x,w',t){\rm Tr}\{T^eU^\dagger(x)U(w')T^e\new{(U^\dagger(w')-U^\dagger(t))(U(x)-U(t))}\}\notag\\
&\qquad-\mathcal K(w,y,t){\rm Tr}\{T^eU^\dagger(w)U(y)T^e\new{(U^\dagger(y)-U^\dagger(t))(U(w)-U(t))}\}\notag\\&\qquad+\mathcal K(w,w',t){\rm Tr}\{T^eU^\dagger(w)U(w')T^e\new{(U^\dagger(w')-U^\dagger(t))(U(w)-U(t))}\}\Big],\notag\\
\frac{dN^{(3)}}{d^2wd^2w'dy}={}&-P\int\Big[\mathcal K(y,y,t){\rm Tr}\{T^e(U^\dagger(x)-U^\dagger(w))U(y)T^e\new{(U^\dagger(y)-U^\dagger(t))(U(y)-U(t))}\}\notag\\
&\qquad-\mathcal K(w',w',t){\rm Tr}\{T^e(U^\dagger(x)-U^\dagger(w))U(w')T^e\new{(U^\dagger(w')-U^\dagger(t))(U(w')-U(t))}\}\Big],\notag\\
\frac{dN^{(4)}}{d^2wd^2w'dy}={}&-P\int\Big[\mathcal K(x,x,t){\rm Tr}\{T^eU^\dagger(x)(U(y)-U(w'))T^e\new{(U^\dagger(x)-U^\dagger(t))(U(x)-U(t))}\}\notag\\
&\qquad-\mathcal K(w,w,t){\rm Tr}\{T^eU^\dagger(w)(U(y)-U(w'))T^e\new{(U^\dagger(w)-U^\dagger(t))(U(w)-U(t))}\}\Big],\notag\\
\frac{dN^{(5)}}{d^2wd^2w'dy}={}&\old{-}\new{+}P\int\Big[\notag\\
&\qquad\mathcal K(y,y,t){\rm Tr}\{(U^\dagger(y)-U^\dagger(t))U(y)T^d\}{\rm Tr}\{(U^\dagger(x)-U^\dagger(w))U(y)T^d\}\notag\\
&\qquad-\mathcal K(w',w',t){\rm Tr}\{(U^\dagger(w')-U^\dagger(t))U(w')T^d\}{\rm Tr}\{(U^\dagger(x)-U^\dagger(w))U(w')T^d\}\notag\\
&\qquad-\mathcal K(x,x,t){\rm Tr}\{(U^\dagger(x)-U^\dagger(t))U(x)T^d\}{\rm Tr}\{U^\dagger(x)(U(y)-U(w'))T^d\}\notag\\
&\qquad+\mathcal K(w,w,t){\rm Tr}\{(U^\dagger(w)-U^\dagger(t))U(w)T^d\}{\rm Tr}\{U^\dagger(w)(U(y)-U(w'))T^d\}\Big],
\label{eq:JIMc}
\end{align}
and $\mathcal J=\sum_{i=1}^5 dN^{(i)}$ (per unit $\delta Y$). After the correction, $dN^{(2)}$ is identical to $dN^{(1)}$ term by term. Numerically, at random points with random adjoint Wilson lines, $\sum_i dN^{(i)}$ equals JIMWLK$\otimes$LO to $2\times10^{-15}$; as sent it differs by 0.22--0.47 (relative).

\subsection{BFKL$\otimes$LO: your formula (correct as sent)}
\begin{align}
\frac{dN}{d^2w\,d^2w'\,dy}\bigg|_{\rm BFKL}={}&-\frac{\alpha_s^2N_c\,\delta Y}{8\pi^6(N_c^2-1)}\int_{x,y,z}\langle\rho^f(x)\rho^f(y)\rangle\Big[\notag\\
&\mathcal K(x-w)\cdot\mathcal K(y-w')K_D(x,y,z){\rm Tr}\{(U^\dagger(x)-U^\dagger(w))(U(y)-U(w'))\}\notag\\
&-2\,\mathcal K(z-w)\cdot\mathcal K(z-w')K(x,y,z){\rm Tr}\{(U^\dagger(z)-U^\dagger(w))(U(z)-U(w'))\}\notag\\
&+\mathcal K(z-w)\cdot\mathcal K(y-w')\big(2K(x,y,z)-K(x,x,z)\big){\rm Tr}\{(U^\dagger(z)-U^\dagger(w))(U(y)-U(w'))\}\notag\\
&+\mathcal K(x-w)\cdot\mathcal K(z-w')\big(2K(x,y,z)-K(y,y,z)\big){\rm Tr}\{(U^\dagger(x)-U^\dagger(w))(U(z)-U(w'))\}\Big],
\label{eq:BFKL}
\end{align}
with $K_D(x,y,z)=K(x,x,z)-2K(x,y,z)+K(y,y,z)$. It agrees with BFKL$\otimes$LO to $8\times10^{-15}$.

%==========================================================================================

\paragraph{Your BFKL formula} (Eq.~(LOBFKL) of your message) is correct as written: with $K_D=K(x,x,z)-2K(x,y,z)+K(y,y,z)$, $\delta Y=\log\frac{\vee}{k^+}$ and the singlet projection above, it equals BFKL$\otimes$LO, i.e.\ the two-$\rho$ sum of Sec.~\ref{sec:bfkl}, to $8.2\times10^{-15}$ (six configurations).

\section{Poles}\label{sec:poles}
With $E(r)=\frac{2}{\epsilon}+2\gamma_E+\log\frac{r^2\mu^2}{4}$:
\begin{itemize}
\item The poles $\times\log\frac{k^+}{\Lambda}$ of Group I Row II, Group I Row V 2b UV and Group II Row IV are in the ratio $1:-2:1$ and cancel ($8.2\times10^{-16}$, three configurations; \texttt{ll\_checks.py poles}).
\item The $\log\frac{\vee}{k^+}$ list has two terms with a pole, (i) and (ii) of Group II Row IV; they cancel exactly. So the BFKL terms are pole free.
\end{itemize}
